     SECTION 0 — EXECUTIVE SUMMARY

<div class="section active" id="exec">
<div class="section-title">Executive Summary</div>
<div class="section-sub">The case for governed AI in intelligence analysis — platform overview, urgent need, and scope of this program.</div>

<!-- Hero stat row -->
<div class="grid grid-4" style="margin-bottom:28px">
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #B84040;text-align:center">
  <div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:800;color:#B84040;line-height:1.1">Now</div>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">AI is already in the intelligence analysis workflow — sanctioned and unsanctioned. The governance question is not whether. It is how fast the governance infrastructure catches up.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1B3A5C;text-align:center">
  <div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:800;color:#1B3A5C;line-height:1.1">23</div>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">THEMIS platform services across 6 namespaces — plus 15 intelligence layer services. Includes Interaction Layer governing prompts, skills, and MCP tool access.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1A9E8C;text-align:center">
  <div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:800;color:#1A9E8C;line-height:1.1">28</div>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">ATHENA interventions targeting sycophancy, automation bias, vigilance decrement, anchoring, and confirmation bias in intelligence analysis.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #6D4FB5;text-align:center">
  <div style="font-size:36px;font-weight:800;color:#6D4FB5;line-height:1.1">21</div>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">Person AI Trust Cell across three specialized teams: Research and Red Team, THEMIS Platform, and Intelligence Layer.</p>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- The Problem -->
<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:20px;margin-bottom:24px">
 <div>
  <h3 class="subsec">The Problem</h3>
  <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.7;margin-bottom:14px">AI adoption in intelligence analysis is not a future event to be planned for. It is a present reality to be managed. Analysts use AI-assisted research tools, large language models for drafting finished intelligence, and AI-summarized source review daily — with or without authorization, and without the infrastructure to make that use safe, accountable, or defensible to oversight.</p>
  <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.7;margin-bottom:14px">The specific failure modes are not theoretical. They emerge structurally from how LLMs behave when deployed without calibration infrastructure in high-stakes analytical environments.</p>
  <div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:10px">
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #B84040;padding:12px"><h4 style="color:#B84040;font-size:12px">Compartment Leakage</h4><p>RAG-based tools surface higher-classification content in sessions scoped to lower classification levels. Without compartment-scoped retrieval enforcement, this is structurally inevitable in any multi-tier intelligence environment.</p></div>
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #B84040;padding:12px"><h4 style="color:#B84040;font-size:12px">Fabricated Source Reporting</h4><p>AI models hallucinate intelligence source references and corroborating reporting with high fluency and apparent confidence. Analysts acting on fabricated sourcing is the most acute analytic failure mode in current AI-assisted workflows.</p></div>
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #B84040;padding:12px"><h4 style="color:#B84040;font-size:12px">Stale Intelligence</h4><p>Analysts present AI-generated assessments without knowing that underlying sources have been superseded, that the source has been compromised, or that the intelligence picture has materially changed since the model's training cutoff or the corpus's last update.</p></div>
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #B84040;padding:12px"><h4 style="color:#B84040;font-size:12px">Uncalibrated Trust</h4><p>Junior analysts with limited experience evaluating AI output accept AI assessments at higher rates than senior analysts — a systematic quality asymmetry invisible to supervision without calibration instrumentation.</p></div>
  </div>
 </div>
 <div>
  <h3 class="subsec">The Urgent Need</h3>
  <p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.7;margin-bottom:14px">Three converging pressures make this urgent rather than important.</p>
  <div class="track-row" style="margin-bottom:10px"><div class="track-label-col" style="background:#B84040;color:#fff;font-size:10px;padding:12px 8px">Professional Liability</div><div class="track-content-col" style="padding:12px 16px"><h4 style="font-size:12px">Oversight findings are already occurring</h4><p>Fabricated source references, unsupported analytic conclusions, and unverifiable AI contributions create oversight exposure. A single significant assessment where AI-generated content was relied upon without verification can lead to flawed policy decisions that are operationally costly.</p></div></div>
  <div class="track-row" style="margin-bottom:10px"><div class="track-label-col" style="background:#9A6800;color:#fff;font-size:10px;padding:12px 8px">Oversight Exposure</div><div class="track-content-col" style="padding:12px 16px"><h4 style="font-size:12px">Congressional and IG oversight is intensifying</h4><p>Intelligence community oversight bodies are actively developing AI accountability frameworks. Agencies that have PCES, PGS, MOIRAI, and ERAS operational before oversight requirements are formalized are compliant on day one. Agencies building these capabilities after the fact are in remediation under scrutiny — explaining past AI use without the infrastructure that would have made it accountable.</p></div></div>
  <div class="track-row"><div class="track-label-col" style="background:#1B3A5C;color:#fff;font-size:10px;padding:12px 8px">Adversarial Risk</div><div class="track-content-col" style="padding:12px 16px"><h4 style="font-size:12px">Adversaries target uncalibrated AI workflows</h4><p>Foreign intelligence services understand that AI-assisted analysis is being used and that it has characteristic failure modes. Corpus poisoning, prompt injection through fabricated open-source reporting, and deliberate targeting of AI anchoring biases are active denial-and-deception techniques. IAS/SCUDO and MAS/EIDOLON exist because adversarial exploitation of AI pipelines is a real collection threat, not a hypothetical one.</p></div></div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- What We've Built -->
<h3 class="subsec">Intelligence Lifecycle Coverage</h3>
<div style="background:#F0F4F8;border-radius:10px;padding:20px 24px;margin-bottom:24px;border:1px solid var(--border)">
 <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(6,1fr);gap:8px;text-align:center">
  <div style="background:#EEF2F8;border-radius:6px;padding:10px 8px;border:1px solid var(--border)"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;margin-bottom:4px">PLANNING</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--sub)">Session intent · Requirement framing · Intention gate</div></div>
  <div style="background:#EEF2F8;border-radius:6px;padding:10px 8px;border:1px solid var(--border)"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;margin-bottom:4px">COLLECTION</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--sub)">PCES compartment scope · IAS adversarial screening · MAS authenticity</div></div>
  <div style="background:#EEF8F6;border-radius:6px;padding:10px 8px;border:1px solid #A0D4CC"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#7DD3C8;margin-bottom:4px">PROCESSING</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--sub)">MOIRAI provenance · TVS validity · RQS retrieval quality</div></div>
  <div style="background:#E6F5F3;border-radius:6px;padding:10px 8px;border:2px solid #1A9E8C"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1A9E8C;margin-bottom:4px">ANALYSIS ← ATHENA</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--sub)">TCS/MIMIR calibration · 28 interventions · Confidence badges</div></div>
  <div style="background:#EEF2F8;border-radius:6px;padding:10px 8px;border:1px solid var(--border)"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;margin-bottom:4px">DISSEMINATION</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--sub)">Dissemination gate · DPS/CODEX provenance · Classification enforcement</div></div>
  <div style="background:#EEF2F8;border-radius:6px;padding:10px 8px;border:1px solid var(--border)"><div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;margin-bottom:4px">FEEDBACK</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--sub)">FGTS corrections · TCS calibration updates · ERAS reasoning audit</div></div>
 </div>
</div>

<h3 class="subsec">What This Program Has Designed</h3>
<div class="grid grid-3" style="margin-bottom:24px">
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1A9E8C">
  <h4 style="color:#1A9E8C">ATHENA</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:10px">Calibrated analysis interface. 28 interventions across 8 categories targeting the specific cognitive failure modes that make AI-assisted analytic work dangerous: sycophancy, automation bias, vigilance decrement, anchoring, and confirmation bias.</p>
  <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px">
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Session intent declaration</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Intention gate</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">8 source type badges</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Calibrated confidence</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Verification queue</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Compliance gaming detection</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Multi-analyst framework</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Pressure-aware session mode</span>
  </div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1B3A5C">
  <h4 style="color:#1B3A5C">THEMIS</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:10px">Trusted Human-AI Enablement for Mission Intelligence and Safety. 23 platform services across 6 namespaces providing governance, accountability, and quality infrastructure across three accountability axes — Origin, Currency, and Trust — plus agent-native capability bounding, coalition handling, and Interaction Layer governance of prompts, skills, and MCP tools. 15 intelligence layer services compound analytical value over time.</p>
  <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px">
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">PCES / AEGIS</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">PGS / NOMOS</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">Provenance / MOIRAI</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">TCS / MIMIR</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">FGTS / ALETHEIA</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">CVS / VERITAS</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">MAS / EIDOLON</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">DPS / CODEX</span>
  </div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #6D4FB5">
  <h4 style="color:#6D4FB5">AI Trust Cell</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;margin-bottom:10px">21-person specialized organization across three teams with a triple mandate: deliver ATHENA and THEMIS, research calibration and human factors science, and govern AI standards across the agency. Research independence is structural, not aspirational.</p>
  <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px">
   <span class="badge" style="background:#F2EEFD;color:#4A2FA8">Research + Red Team · 7</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF3FF;color:#1A3A80">Platform Team · 7</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0A4A40">Intelligence Team · 6</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#F2EEFD;color:#4A2FA8">Max 40% delivery capacity</span>
   <span class="badge" style="background:#F2EEFD;color:#4A2FA8">Research track protected</span>
  </div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- Roadmap -->
<h3 class="subsec">Three Accountability Axes + A Proposed Fourth</h3>
<p style="font-size:13px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.7;margin-bottom:18px">Every AI-assisted analytic interaction in THEMIS is governed across three independent dimensions. An assessment can fail on any one of them — a provenance-perfect analysis of stale intelligence is still a dangerous product. The three axes are architecturally enforced independently so that failure on one does not mask failure on another.</p>
<div class="grid grid-3" style="margin-bottom:24px;gap:16px">

 <!-- ORIGIN -->
 <div style="border-radius:10px;border:2px solid #1B3A5C;overflow:hidden">
  <div style="background:#1B3A5C;padding:14px 18px">
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,.6);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.12em;margin-bottom:4px">Axis I</div>
   <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:#fff;margin-bottom:2px">Origin</div>
   <div style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.75)">Where did this claim come from?</div>
  </div>
  <div style="padding:16px 18px;background:#fff">
   <p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65;margin-bottom:12px">Every claim in every ATHENA response has a traceable origin. Retrieved from a specific source document. Derived from model training weights with no retrieval. Synthesized across partial sources. Generated from a media artifact with its own accuracy profile. The origin type determines the verification action, the confidence ceiling, and the FGTS routing — and it is made visible to the analyst before they act on the claim.</p>
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:8px">Services</div>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-bottom:12px">
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">Provenance / MOIRAI</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">PCES / AEGIS</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">DPS / CODEX</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">ERAS / LOGOS</span>
   </div>
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:8px">Source type badges</div>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px">
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #2E5FAD;color:#2E5FAD;background:#EEF3FF">GRND</span>
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #9A6800;color:#9A6800;background:#FFF7E8">PARAM</span>
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #6B7590;color:#6B7590;background:#F4F6FA">SYNTH</span>
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #0D6B5E;color:#0D6B5E;background:#E6F5F3">TRANSCRIPT</span>
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #4A2FA8;color:#4A2FA8;background:#EEEBFF">VIDEO</span>
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #6D4FB5;color:#6D4FB5;background:#F2EEFD">AUDIO</span>
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #8B2222;color:#8B2222;background:#FBF0F0">IMAGE</span>
    <span style="font-size:9.5px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid #7A5000;color:#7A5000;background:#FFF3E0">OCR</span>
   </div>
   <div style="margin-top:12px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:10px"><strong style="color:var(--navy)">Why it matters:</strong> An AI system produces uniformly fluent output regardless of whether a claim was retrieved, inferred, or synthesized from partial data. Without origin visibility, analysts treat all claims as equally grounded. GRND and PARAM claims require different verification — conflating them creates systematic analytic error that is invisible without instrumentation.</div>
  </div>
 </div>

 <!-- CURRENCY -->
 <div style="border-radius:10px;border:2px solid #0D6B5E;overflow:hidden">
  <div style="background:#0D6B5E;padding:14px 18px">
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,.6);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.12em;margin-bottom:4px">Axis II</div>
   <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:#fff;margin-bottom:2px">Currency</div>
   <div style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.75)">Is this intelligence still valid?</div>
  </div>
  <div style="padding:16px 18px;background:#fff">
   <p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65;margin-bottom:12px">Intelligence decays. Sources are compromised, programs are cancelled, assessments are superseded by new collection. A claim that was accurate when its source was ingested may be dangerously wrong today. THEMIS tracks the temporal validity of every source in the corpus — applying decay functions by source type, propagating invalidation events from external monitoring through the provenance graph to every derived claim, and surfacing currency warnings before analysts act on stale intelligence.</p>
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:8px">Services</div>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-bottom:12px">
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">TVS / KAIROS</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">KCS / ARGUS</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">MDS / KRONOS</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">CVS / VERITAS</span>
   </div>
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:8px">Currency mechanisms</div>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;margin-bottom:12px">
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#0D6B5E;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">TVS</span>Continuous temporal decay scoring per source. Configurable half-life by intelligence type — current reporting decays faster than foundational assessments.</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#0D6B5E;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">KCS</span>External feed monitoring — OSINT, allied reporting, collection system status. Fires ActiveInvalidationRequests to TVS when a source is superseded or compromised.</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#0D6B5E;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">MDS</span>Model version drift detection. When the underlying model changes between sessions, prior calibration may no longer apply — MDS surfaces this before the analyst proceeds.</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#0D6B5E;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">CVS</span>Generation-time source corroboration against IC databases. Hard interrupt if a cited source cannot be corroborated — the claim is blocked before the analyst sees it.</div>
   </div>
   <div style="margin-top:2px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:10px"><strong style="color:var(--navy)">Why it matters:</strong> Stale intelligence presented with current-intelligence confidence is one of the most dangerous failure modes in any collection-driven analytic system. An analyst with no visibility into source validity cannot distinguish a current assessment from one that was accurate two years ago before a fundamental change in the target's program.</div>
  </div>
 </div>

 <!-- TRUST -->
 <div style="border-radius:10px;border:2px solid #1A9E8C;overflow:hidden">
  <div style="background:#1A9E8C;padding:14px 18px">
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,.6);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.12em;margin-bottom:4px">Axis III</div>
   <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:800;color:#fff;margin-bottom:2px">Trust</div>
   <div style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.75)">Is the analyst's reliance calibrated?</div>
  </div>
  <div style="padding:16px 18px;background:#fff">
   <p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65;margin-bottom:12px">Knowing where a claim came from and whether its source is current is necessary but not sufficient. The analyst must be appropriately calibrated to the AI's actual accuracy on this type of claim in this domain. An analyst who over-relies on AI in domains where it performs poorly, or who under-utilizes AI in domains where it performs well, is producing worse analysis than the AI's accuracy warrants. TCS/MIMIR measures this calibration continuously and the 28 ATHENA interventions are designed to correct it.</p>
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:8px">Services</div>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-bottom:12px">
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">TCS / MIMIR</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">FGTS / ALETHEIA</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">RQS / HERMES</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">ERAS / LOGOS</span>
   </div>
   <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:8px">Trust mechanisms</div>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;margin-bottom:12px">
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">TCS</span>Domain-specific Bayesian calibration per analyst. Reliance-Accuracy Index tracks whether each analyst is trusting the AI at the right level — by claim type, domain, and model version.</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">FGTS</span>Verified analyst corrections accumulate as ground truth. 5-factor weighting ensures only high-quality corrections update the calibration model. The corpus improves with every analyst interaction.</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">RQS</span>Retrieval quality monitoring. Precision, miss detection, and embedding drift tracking ensure the retrieval layer is surfacing relevant intelligence rather than degrading silently.</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700;flex-shrink:0">ERAS</span>Per-turn reasoning capture. Unsupported claim detection flags conclusions with no retrieval backing. Full audit trail for oversight body review.</div>
   </div>
   <div style="margin-top:2px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;border-top:1px solid var(--border);padding-top:10px"><strong style="color:var(--navy)">Why it matters:</strong> AI calibration is not a fixed property of the model — it is a relationship between a specific analyst, a specific claim type, and a specific domain. An analyst well-calibrated for technical intelligence may be systematically over-reliant on AI for geopolitical assessment. Without per-analyst, per-domain calibration measurement, this variation is invisible to supervisors and self-invisible to analysts.</div>
  </div>
 </div>

</div>

<!-- Proposed fourth axis teaser -->
<div style="background:#F5F0FF;border-radius:10px;border:2px dashed #9B7FD4;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div style="background:#6B3FA8;padding:10px 18px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
  <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:rgba(255,255,255,.6);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.12em">Proposed · Addendum D</span>
  <span style="font-size:16px;font-weight:800;color:#fff">Competence</span>
  <span style="font-size:12px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.75);margin-left:4px">Can this AI system reliably perform this task at all?</span>
 </div>
 <div style="padding:14px 18px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px">
  <div>
   <p style="font-size:12px;color:#3A1A70;line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:8px">The three current axes assume the AI can produce reliable output and ask how well it has done so. The Competence axis asks a prior question: is reliable output achievable on this task at all? An assessment can pass Origin, Currency, and Trust — provenance-perfect, current, well-calibrated — and still be unreliable if the task exceeds the model's actual capability.</p>
   <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:#5A3A90;line-height:1.55"><strong>Six failure modes:</strong> knowledge boundary violations, task-capability mismatch, out-of-distribution queries, compound capability degradation, epistemic non-declaration, and adversarial competence probing.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <div style="font-size:10.5px;font-weight:700;color:#6B3FA8;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">Proposed services</div>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;margin-bottom:8px">
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EDE8FF;color:#4A2FA8">CPS / APORIA — Capability Profiling</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EDE8FF;color:#4A2FA8">ODS / LETHE — OOD Screening</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EDE8FF;color:#4A2FA8">IAS/SCUDO probe detection</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EDE8FF;color:#4A2FA8">ERAS reasoning pattern detection</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EDE8FF;color:#4A2FA8">TCS capability-stratified calibration</span>
   </div>
   <p style="font-size:11px;color:#5A3A90;line-height:1.55">Partially buildable now. Hard enforcement gated on six open research questions — including reliable epistemic self-assessment in LLMs and OOD detection for autoregressive generative models. See Competence Axis tab for full specification.</p>
  </div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- Axis interaction callout -->
<div style="background:#F4F7FB;border-radius:8px;padding:16px 20px;border:1px solid var(--border);margin-bottom:20px">
 <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">Why all three axes must be enforced independently</div>
 <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:14px">
  <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6"><span style="font-weight:700;color:#8B2222">Origin without Currency:</span> The analyst knows this claim came from a retrieved source — but the source was superseded six months ago. Provenance-perfect, lethally stale.</div>
  <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6"><span style="font-weight:700;color:#8B2222">Currency without Trust:</span> The source is current — but the analyst is systematically over-reliant on AI in this domain and has not verified a single high-confidence PARAM claim this session. The intelligence picture is current but the analytic judgment is degraded.</div>
  <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6"><span style="font-weight:700;color:#8B2222">Trust without Origin:</span> The analyst is well-calibrated — but cannot see that the claim they just accepted is SYNTH (synthesized across partial sources with no single direct retrieval). Calibration cannot operate on hidden information.</div>
 </div>
</div>

<h3 class="subsec">17-Month Implementation Roadmap</h3>
<div class="grid grid-4">
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1B3A5C">
  <span class="badge" style="background:#1B3A5C;color:#fff">Phase 1–2 · Weeks 1–8</span>
  <h4 style="margin-top:10px;color:#1B3A5C">Safety Gates</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px">PCES/AEGIS and PGS/NOMOS deployed. No AI interaction touches client data without compartment enforcement and policy guardrails. Classification log generation automated. Intelligence Oversight Board constituted.</p>
  <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:11px;color:#8B2222;font-weight:600">Eliminates acute privilege and policy liability from day one.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #0D6B5E">
  <span class="badge" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff">Phase 3–4 · Weeks 9–28</span>
  <h4 style="margin-top:10px;color:#0D6B5E">Core Infrastructure</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px">Provenance/MOIRAI with cryptographic attestation. TCS/MIMIR calibration baselines. CVS/VERITAS source corroboration. MDS/KRONOS model drift detection. DPS/CODEX document provenance.</p>
  <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:11px;color:#8B2222;font-weight:600">Complete chain of custody for every AI interaction. Answerable to any audit.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #9A6800">
  <span class="badge" style="background:#9A6800;color:#fff">Phase 5–6 · Weeks 29–46</span>
  <h4 style="margin-top:10px;color:#9A6800">Quality Loops</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px">FGTS/ALETHEIA with quality weighting. TVS/KAIROS validity propagation. IAS/SCUDO adversarial screening. MAS/EIDOLON media authenticity. Proprietary ground truth corpus begins.</p>
  <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:11px;color:#8B2222;font-weight:600">Platform AI calibration improves with every analyst correction on agency-specific requirements.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #4A2FA8">
  <span class="badge" style="background:#4A2FA8;color:#fff">Phase 7–8 · Weeks 47–66</span>
  <h4 style="margin-top:10px;color:#4A2FA8">Intelligence and Audit</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px">KCS/ARGUS external currency. RQS/HERMES retrieval observability. ERAS/LOGOS reasoning auditability. TVS ground truth corpus validity extension.</p>
  <p style="margin-top:8px;font-size:11px;color:#8B2222;font-weight:600">Full tradecraft compliance audit surface. Answerable to any Congressional or IG oversight inquiry.</p>
 </div>
</div>

<div class="divider"></div>
<div style="background:#EEF2F8;border-radius:8px;padding:20px 24px;border-left:4px solid #1B3A5C;border:1px solid #C0CEDF">
 <h4 style="font-size:14px;margin-bottom:8px;color:#1B3A5C">The Bottom Line</h4>
 <p style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--sub)">An agency without THEMIS is using AI on client matters without knowing what privilege boundaries were crossed, what evidence has been superseded, whether analysts are trusting AI appropriately, or whether the reasoning behind AI-assisted conclusions can be defended. An agency with THEMIS has turned that exposure into a governance posture — and that posture into a competitive position. The safety gates alone justify the first eight weeks. Every phase beyond that builds a compounding institutional advantage that is not replicable from outside the agency.</p>
</div>
</div>

<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION 1 — PLATFORM SERVICES
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<div class="section" id="platform">
<div class="section-title">THEMIS Platform Services</div>
<div class="section-sub">23 services across 6 deployment namespaces. 9 original + 5 Addendum A + 3 agent-native + 3 Addendum F core + 3 Interaction Layer (PRS, SKS, MGS). Intelligence cycle completion in Intelligence Layer tab. See also: 15 intelligence layer services.</div>



<div id="svc-grid"></div>

<h3 class="subsec">Architectural Extensions (No new services)</h3>
<div class="grid grid-3" id="ext-grid"></div>
</div>


<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION 2 — INTELLIGENCE LAYER
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<div class="section" id="intelligence">
<div class="section-title">THEMIS Intelligence Layer</div>
<div class="section-sub">Fifteen services across five namespaces: <strong>themis-knowledge</strong> (semantic foundation, memory, document diff), <strong>themis-research</strong> (orchestration, patterns, anticipatory), <strong>themis-warning</strong> (temporal, anomaly, unknown unknowns), <strong>themis-dissemination</strong> (policymaker delivery), <strong>themis-requirements</strong> (tasking integration). Year 2–4 build sequence. Each namespace depends on OGS/YGGDRASIL semantic foundation. Proprietary to the intelligence community — built on its own assessment history, source reliability corpus, and institutional knowledge.</div>

<!-- Strategic framing -->
<div style="background:#EEF2F8;border-radius:8px;padding:20px 24px;border:1px solid #C0CEDF;margin-bottom:28px">
 <h4 style="font-size:14px;margin-bottom:10px;color:#1B3A5C">The Strategic Shift</h4>
 <p style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--sub);margin-bottom:10px">The THEMIS governance foundation answers: <em>was this AI use appropriate and accountable?</em> The intelligence layer answers: <em>what does the intelligence community know, what should it assess, and what collection does it need before it knows to ask?</em></p>
 <p style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;color:var(--sub)">Governance infrastructure is necessary but not sufficient for competitive advantage. Every firm that builds THEMIS Phase 1–5 will have a governed AI platform. These six services are what make the platform proprietary — they are built on the agency's own assessment history, correction corpus, and institutional knowledge. No vendor can replicate them. They compound in value with every matter the agency handles.</p>
</div>
