<!-- ── CRYPTOGRAPHIC ATTESTATION ───────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec" id="tr-crypto" style="margin-top:32px">Cryptographic Attestation Design</h3>
<div style="background:#FBF0F0;border:1px solid #E8B4B4;border-radius:8px;padding:14px 18px;margin-bottom:18px">
 <h4 style="color:#8B2222;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:6px">Phase 3-4 hard constraint</h4>
 <p style="font-size:12px;color:#6B1818;line-height:1.6">Cryptographic attestation must be implemented in Phase 3-4 alongside the Provenance/MOIRAI core build. An unsigned event cannot be retroactively signed without breaking the hash chain. A chain with mixed signed/unsigned events provides weaker guarantees than a fully signed chain. There is no retrofit path — the first analyst interactions are cold start by definition, and those initial events must be signed from inception.</p>
</div>

<div class="grid grid-3" style="margin-bottom:20px">
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1B3A5C">
  <h4 style="color:#1B3A5C">Event Signing via Vault</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.6">Each THEMIS service has an asymmetric key pair managed by Vault. Services never hold private keys — they call Vault's signing API with the event payload and receive a signature. MOIRAI validates signatures at the write boundary and rejects unsigned or invalid events. Key rotation does not invalidate prior events: signing_key_id is stored with each event and the public key registry retains all historical keys after rotation.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #0D6B5E">
  <h4 style="color:#0D6B5E">SHA-256 Hash Chaining</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.6">Every event includes a hash of the prior event in the chain (prev_event_hash). Any tampering with a prior event breaks the hash of every subsequent event. An attacker cannot insert, modify, or delete any event without the break propagating forward and becoming detectable. MOIRAI validates the hash chain on write — events whose prev_event_hash does not match the actual prior event hash are rejected.</p>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #9A6800">
  <h4 style="color:#9A6800">RFC 3161 External Timestamping</h4>
  <p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.6">Every 24 hours, MOIRAI submits a chain digest to an RFC 3161 compliant timestamp authority and stores the response as an anchor event. External parties can verify the chain existed in a specific state at a specific time without THEMIS infrastructure access. A compromised MOIRAI can forge events after an anchor but cannot alter events before it without breaking the hash chain at the anchor point.</p>
 </div>
</div>

<h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:10px">Event Schema Extension — 4 New Fields on Every Event</h4>
<table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:20px">
 <tr><th>Field</th><th>Type</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
 <tr><td><code>signature</code></td><td>string</td><td>Cryptographic signature over the canonical event payload. Produced by the originating service via Vault signing API.</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>signing_key_id</code></td><td>string</td><td>Identifies which key pair signed this event. Used to select the correct historical public key from the registry for verification. Valid after key rotation.</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>prev_event_hash</code></td><td>string (SHA-256)</td><td>Hash of the prior event in the service's event stream. Creates the tamper-evident chain. MOIRAI validates on write — mismatch causes rejection.</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>tsa_token</code></td><td>string (nullable)</td><td>RFC 3161 timestamp authority token. Present on 24h TSA anchor events. Absent on regular events — regular events are covered by the nearest subsequent anchor.</td></tr>
</table>

<h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:10px">Verification API — 3 Modes</h4>
<div class="grid grid-3" style="margin-bottom:20px">
 <div class="card"><h4 style="color:#1B3A5C">Single Event</h4><p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">Verify one event's signature, payload integrity, and hash chain position. Used for spot audit queries.</p></div>
 <div class="card"><h4 style="color:#1B3A5C">Range Verification</h4><p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">Walk the hash chain between two event IDs, confirming no events were inserted, deleted, or modified within the range.</p></div>
 <div class="card"><h4 style="color:#1B3A5C">Full Chain Audit</h4><p style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px">Verify every event in a session or requirement scope, checking all TSA anchor points. Produces a signed audit certificate suitable for court or oversight body submission.</p></div>
</div>

<h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">Services Required to Sign</h4>
<p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);margin-bottom:10px">All 22 THEMIS platform services plus the inference gateway and claim extractor. No event reaches MOIRAI unsigned.</p>
<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-bottom:24px">
 <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">PCES/AEGIS</span><span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">PGS/NOMOS</span><span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Provenance/MOIRAI</span><span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">TCS/MIMIR</span><span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">DPS/CODEX</span><span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">FGTS/ALETHEIA</span><span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">TVS/KAIROS</span><span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">RQS/HERMES</span><span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">CVS/VERITAS</span><span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">IAS/SCUDO</span><span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">MAS/EIDOLON</span><span class="badge" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">KCS/ARGUS</span><span class="badge" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">ERAS/LOGOS</span><span class="badge" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">MDS/KRONOS</span><span class="badge" style="background:#F2F5FA;color:#5A6480">Inference Gateway</span><span class="badge" style="background:#F2F5FA;color:#5A6480">Claim Extractor</span>
</div>

<!-- ── SERVICE SPECIFICATIONS ──────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec" id="tr-specs">Service Technical Specifications</h3>
<p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--sub);margin-bottom:16px">All 22 THEMIS platform services organized by 7 namespaces. Click each service to expand storage architecture, latency targets, fallback behavior, and key constraints.</p>

<div class="layer-header" style="background:#1B3A5C;color:#fff;margin-bottom:10px">Safety Gates — themis-gates</div>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#1B3A5C;font-size:14px">PCES</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">PCES / AEGIS — Compartment and Classification Enforcement</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">Phase 1-2</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Position and Trigger</h5><p>First in every request path — before PGS, before retrieval, before inference. Every ATHENA interaction passes PCES before any other THEMIS service is invoked. No fallback: PCES failure is a hard stop. No AI interaction proceeds without compartment clearance.</p>
   <h5>Classification Schema</h5><p>UNCLASSIFIED, CONFIDENTIAL, SECRET, TOP_SECRET, TS_SCI, SAP. Requirement scope enforced as a hard boundary — source intelligence from Requirement A cannot appear in sessions scoped to Requirement B. CoI detection cross-references collection personnel against the cleared analyst registry at session open.</p>
   <h5>Latency Target</h5><p>P99 &lt; 50ms. PCES is in the synchronous critical path for every interaction. Cache-first: active requirement scope and compartment classifications cached in Redis per session. Cold lookup from PostgreSQL only on cache miss or session boundary.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Requirement scope registry</td><td>Redis (TTL: session)</td><td>Active requirement compartment classifications per analyst session. Cache-first for latency.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Classification log</td><td>PostgreSQL (append-only)</td><td>Immutable audit record of every compartment classification decision. Every event signed.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Consent records</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Per-source consent state and collection authority grants. Immutable append-only for compliance.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>CoI registry</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Conflict-of-interest records cross-referenced against firm cleared personnel registry at session open.</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#1B3A5C;font-size:14px">PGS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">PGS / NOMOS — Policy and Guardrails Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">Phase 1-2</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Position and Trigger</h5><p>Second in every request path — after PCES compartment clearance. Every compartment-cleared interaction passes PGS before reaching retrieval or inference. Fail-safe default: PGS failure routes to the most restrictive policy. No interaction proceeds on PGS unavailability.</p>
   <h5>Interaction Classes</h5><p>intelligence_analysis, finished_intelligence, research, source debriefing_prep, expert_briefing, and others. Each class carries configurable constraints: CVS mode (blocking vs advisory), dissemination gate thresholds, counter-position requirement, Deadline-Critical hardening eligibility. Interaction class changes require Intelligence Oversight Board approval.</p>
   <h5>Policy Rule Engine</h5><p>YAML-authored rules in a version-controlled policy store. Rule changes are PRed, reviewed by Intelligence Oversight Board, and deployed through a formal change process. PGS policy version is logged in every session manifest and provenance record — enabling policy-stratified audit queries.</p>
   <h5>Latency Target</h5><p>P99 &lt; 100ms. Policy rule evaluation is in-memory against the cached rule set. Rule set refreshed from the version-controlled store on PGS service restart and on Intelligence Oversight Board policy deployment.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Policy rule store</td><td>Git-versioned YAML</td><td>Authoritative policy rules per interaction class. Every change is a versioned commit with Intelligence Oversight Board sign-off.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Active rule cache</td><td>In-memory</td><td>Current policy rule set loaded at startup. Near-zero evaluation latency. Refreshed on policy deployment.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Interaction log</td><td>PostgreSQL (append-only)</td><td>Per-interaction classification result, policy version applied, PII detection outcome. Every event signed.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Dissemination gate log</td><td>PostgreSQL (append-only)</td><td>Export decisions, unverified claim counts, Deadline-Critical hardening activations, supervisor acknowledgments.</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<div class="layer-header" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff;margin-bottom:10px">Core Infrastructure — themis-core</div>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#0D6B5E;font-size:14px">MOIRAI</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">Provenance / MOIRAI — AI Content Provenance</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Phase 3-4</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Write Path</h5><p>Validates cryptographic signature before accepting any event. Validates prev_event_hash against the actual prior event hash — mismatch causes rejection. Appends to Neo4j provenance graph and append-only event log. All 22 THEMIS platform services plus infrastructure components sign events via Vault before submission.</p>
   <h5>Graph Model</h5><p>Nodes: session, turn, claim, chunk, document, analyst, model_version, corpus_snapshot. Edges: produced, retrieved, verified, derived_from, attributed_to, superseded_by. Multi-analyst attribution: multiple analyst nodes per session. Matter-level cross-session query index for Intervention 28.</p>
   <h5>Fallback</h5><p>MOIRAI unavailability does not stop ATHENA sessions — event-writing services buffer to Kafka. MOIRAI processes buffered events on recovery with full replay capability. Sessions during unavailability are marked with a MOIRAI_BUFFERED flag in the session manifest.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Provenance graph</td><td>Neo4j (clustered)</td><td>Primary DAG. DAG traversal, claim lineage, requirement-level cross-session queries. Partitioned by matter ID.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Event log</td><td>PostgreSQL (append-only)</td><td>Immutable event record for audit. Signed events with hash chain. Partitioned by date and matter.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Event buffer</td><td>Kafka consumer group</td><td>Receives all signed events from all services. Processed in order. Replay-capable on MOIRAI recovery.</td></tr>
   </table>
   <h5 style="margin-top:10px">Latency Targets</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Operation</th><th>Target</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Event write (async)</td><td>P95 &lt; 200ms</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Claim provenance query</td><td>P95 &lt; 500ms</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Full chain audit</td><td>Async — minutes for large matters</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#0D6B5E;font-size:14px">TCS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">TCS / MIMIR — Trust Calibration Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Phase 3-4</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Calibration Model Architecture</h5><p>Domain-specific Bayesian posterior per (claim_type, domain, model_version, source_type) tuple. Prior initialized from vendor benchmarks at cold start. Updated from weighted FGTS correction signals. Separate calibration stream per modality (text, video, audio, image, OCR) — analyst calibration for document analysis does not transfer to video analysis.</p>
   <h5>RAI Engine — 7 Behavioral Input Streams</h5><p>Verification completion rate · Episode follow-through rate · Reformulation quality score · Counter-position engagement · Gaming probability score (inverted) · Pressure mode adjustment · Supervisory confirmation rate. Streams combined into Reliance-Accuracy Index per analyst per domain.</p>
   <h5>Fallback</h5><p>On TCS unavailability, ATHENA displays PRIOR: MED badges (conservative fallback, not model self-report). Sessions continue with degraded calibration signal. Fallback state is visible in session manifest.</p>
   <h5>Latency Target</h5><p>Score serving from Redis P95 &lt; 50ms. Cache miss falls back to PostgreSQL query P95 &lt; 200ms. Calibration model updates batch every 15 minutes from FGTS correction stream.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Calibration models</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Bayesian posterior per (claim_type, domain, model_version, source_type). Versioned with history.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Score cache</td><td>Redis (TTL: 15 min)</td><td>Calibrated confidence scores per tuple. Primary serve path for ATHENA badge requests.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>RAI records</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Per-analyst RAI history by domain. Time-series for longitudinal calibration analysis.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Gaming score log</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Per-session gaming probability score with 4-signal breakdown. Fed by behavioral telemetry.</td></tr>
   </table>
   <h5 style="margin-top:10px">Cold Start State Machine</h5>
   <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;margin-top:4px">
    <span class="badge" style="background:#F4F6FA;color:#6B7590">UNINITIALIZED</span>
    <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);align-self:center">→</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">PRIOR ONLY</span>
    <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);align-self:center">→</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#EEF3FF;color:#2E5FAD">CALIBRATING</span>
    <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);align-self:center">→</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">CALIBRATED</span>
    <span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);align-self:center">→</span>
    <span class="badge" style="background:#FBF0F0;color:#8B2222">RE-CALIBRATING</span>
   </div>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#0D6B5E;font-size:14px">DPS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">DPS / CODEX — Document Provenance Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Phase 3-4</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Document Lifecycle Events</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Event</th><th>DPS Action</th></tr>

    <tr><td><code>ContentEditedEvent</code></td><td>Edit depth score (embedding distance AI→human revision). Routed to FGTS.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>DocumentOpenedEvent</code></td><td>TVS/KAIROS currency check for all source document IDs in provenance map.</td></tr>
    <tr><td><code>DocumentFinalizedEvent</code></td><td>Complete provenance snapshot: claims, verification states, TVS validity, TCS RAI snapshot at finalization.</td></tr>

   <div style="background:#FBF0F0;border-radius:4px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:11.5px;color:#6B1818;line-height:1.5">DPS document-level event schemas must be designed in Phase 3-4 alongside MOIRAI. No retrofit path — matter records created before DPS exists lack document-layer provenance that cannot be reconstructed.</div>
  </div>
  <div>

    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>

    <tr><td>Provenance panel cache</td><td>Redis (TTL: session)</td><td>Real-time document provenance state per document ID. Queried by authoring solution on every paragraph focus.</td></tr>
   </table>
   <h5 style="margin-top:10px">API</h5><p>Synchronous REST for provenance panel queries — <strong>P95 &lt; 100ms</strong>. Async webhook for document lifecycle events from authoring solution. OpenAPI schema versioned. The authoring solution is a DPS client — DPS handles all THEMIS interactions on the authoring solution's behalf.</p>

<div class="layer-header" style="background:#9A6800;color:#fff;margin-bottom:10px">Quality Layer — themis-quality</div>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#9A6800;font-size:14px">FGTS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">FGTS / ALETHEIA — Feedback and Ground Truth Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 5-6</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Correction Confidence Weighting (5-factor)</h5><p>Domain RAI · Gaming score (inverted) · Pressure mode multiplier · Supervisory confirmation multiplier · Peer agreement multiplier. Corrections below minimum weight threshold → human review queue. High gaming score reduces weight of all corrections from that session.</p>
   <h5>Human Review Queue — 3 Trigger Conditions</h5><p>1. Computed weight below minimum threshold. 2. Correction contradicts a prior high-confidence correction on the same claim type and domain. 3. HIGH confidence badge being marked Incorrect — the highest-consequence correction type. Reviewed by Corpus Quality Specialist. Accept / Reject / Escalate workflow.</p>
   <h5>Corpus Versioning</h5><p>Weekly snapshots stored as signed events in Provenance/MOIRAI. Rollback by selecting prior snapshot and recomputing TCS calibration without excluded corrections. Tractable because FGTS uses weighted accumulation, not gradient descent.</p>
   <h5>Latency Target</h5><p>Correction event processing P95 &lt; 2 seconds. Human review queue updated in real-time. TCS calibration model update batch every 15 minutes from correction stream.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Ground truth corpus</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Weighted analyst correction records by (claim_type, domain, model_version, source_type). Primary calibration data for TCS.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Corpus snapshots</td><td>PostgreSQL + object store</td><td>Weekly versioned snapshots for rollback. Each snapshot is a signed MOIRAI event.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Human review queue</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Flagged corrections pending Corpus Quality Specialist review. Accept/reject/escalate decisions logged.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Correction event log</td><td>Kafka → MOIRAI</td><td>CorrectionEvents signed and published. Immutable audit record of all corrections and their confidence weights.</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#9A6800;font-size:14px">TVS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">TVS / KAIROS — Temporal Validity Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 5-6</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Decay Model</h5><p>Configurable decay functions per document type. Different half-life parameters: foundational assessments (longer), current intelligence (medium), tactical reporting (shorter), source debriefing (configurable by source reliability rating). Decay is continuous — validity score degrades over time absent explicit invalidation events.</p>
   <h5>DAG Propagation</h5><p>When a source is invalidated via ActiveInvalidationRequest from KCS, TVS walks the MOIRAI provenance DAG to find all claims derived from that source and propagates ValidityChangeEvents. Propagation is async — seconds to minutes depending on requirement scope and DAG depth.</p>
   <h5>Ground Truth Corpus Extension (Phase 7-8)</h5><p>FGTS corpus items registered with TVS alongside active requirement evidence. When KCS invalidates a source underlying a corpus item, TVS fires CorpusItemValidityDecayEvent to FGTS, routing the item to the human review queue. Separate decay profiles for corpus items vs. evidence (corpus items have longer half-life).</p>
   <h5>Fallback</h5><p>On TVS unavailability, ATHENA shows validity state from cache with a "validity status may be stale" warning. DPS provenance panel flags source validity as unavailable on DocumentOpenedEvent.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Validity records</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Validity score per source document with decay parameters, last-evaluated timestamp, invalidation event history.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Corpus validity records</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>FGTS corpus item validity — separate table with corpus-specific decay profiles.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Validity score cache</td><td>Redis (TTL: 1h)</td><td>Current validity score per source ID. Primary serve path for ATHENA badge requests. Refreshed on invalidation event.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Point-in-time index</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Historical validity state at any timestamp. Enables audit of what validity scores were at any historical moment.</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#9A6800;font-size:14px">RQS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">RQS / HERMES — Retrieval Quality Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 5-6</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Function</h5><p>Observability and feedback service for the retrieval pipeline. No direct analyst-facing surface — surfaces in the ATHENA Context Window Inspector (chunk match scores per retrieved chunk) and the Intelligence Oversight Board quality dashboard.</p>
   <h5>Precision Measurement</h5><p>Rolling precision per query class computed from FGTS verification outcomes: verified claims from retrieved chunks are true positives; "Not in sources" corrections are misses. Precision tracked by (query class, domain, corpus version) to detect retrieval quality degradation.</p>
   <h5>Miss Detection</h5><p>Identifies queries where retrieved chunks do not support the generated claims. Cross-references FGTS "Not in sources" outcomes with the retrieval trace to identify specific chunk/query combinations producing synthesis confabulation. These are routed to FGTS as synthesis failure signals and to the retrieval team as tuning signals.</p>
   <h5>TVS-Retrieval Intersection</h5><p>Flags queries where low-validity source documents (TVS score below threshold) were retrieved and used. These are quality failures independent of whether the model correctly characterized the low-validity source.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Quality metrics</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Rolling precision per (query class, domain, corpus version). Time-series for trend analysis.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Miss log</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Per-query miss events with chunk/query identifiers. Used for retrieval tuning and FGTS signal routing.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Embedding drift records</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Cosine similarity distribution snapshots over time. Drift detection triggers retrieval quality alerts.</td></tr>
   </table>
   <h5 style="margin-top:10px">Embedding Drift Monitoring</h5><p>Monitors cosine similarity distributions in retrieval results over rolling windows. Significant distribution shift indicates corpus drift or embedding model degradation. Alert routed to Engineering Lead and Intelligence ML Lead for investigation.</p>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#9A6800;font-size:14px">CVS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">CVS / VERITAS — Source Corroboration Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 3-4</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Trigger and Mode</h5><p>Fires on every model response before rendering. Mode configurable per PGS/NOMOS interaction class: <strong>mandatory-blocking</strong> for intelligence_analysis and finished_intelligence; <strong>advisory</strong> for research classes. Blocking mode holds the response until citation resolution completes or a No Corroboration hard interrupt fires.</p>
   <h5>Latency Target</h5><p>P95 &lt; 2 seconds for responses containing up to 10 citations. No Corroboration cases are synchronous and blocking — the interrupt fires immediately when the first No Corroboration result returns.</p>
   <h5>Fallback</h5><p>If both IC intelligence databases APIs are unavailable, CVS surfaces an advisory rather than blocking. Interaction logged with <code>verification_status: service_unavailable</code>. PGS/NOMOS can be configured to block export on service_unavailable sessions for high-consequence classes.</p>
   <h5>External API Security</h5><p>Credentials managed through Vault. Only source reference strings transmitted to IC intelligence databases — no intelligence content, no context, no user identity. PCES requirement scope constrains which databases CVS may query.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Resolution cache</td><td>Redis</td><td>TTL: 24h Verified, 4h Unresolved, 1h No Corroboration. Cache hit avoids IC intelligence databases round-trip.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Event log</td><td>Kafka → MOIRAI</td><td>CitationVerificationEvent per citation checked. Signed. Includes resolution status, latency, API used.</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#9A6800;font-size:14px">IAS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">IAS / SCUDO — Input Adversarial Screening Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 5-6</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Pipeline Position</h5><p>At retrieval ingestion, before chunks enter the retrieval candidate pool. Text chunking of non-adversarial chunks continues in parallel — IAS does not block the text pipeline for clean chunks. Only quarantine-disposition chunks are synchronously blocking.</p>
   <h5>Detection Layers (3, all run per chunk)</h5>
   <p><strong>1. Signature matching</strong> — against IAS injection taxonomy. Taxonomy maintained by Red Team Evaluator (Platform Adversarial), reviewed quarterly by Intelligence Oversight Board.<br>
   <strong>2. Embedding similarity</strong> — against adversarial injection pattern corpus. Cosine similarity above threshold triggers flagged/quarantined disposition.<br>
   <strong>3. Heuristic scoring</strong> — on imperative-directive linguistic patterns: role-reassignment language, system prompt override attempts, jailbreak signature strings.</p>
   <h5>Risk Thresholds</h5><p>Configurable by PGS policy per requirement class. Default: Advisory 0.3–0.6, Flagged 0.6–0.85, Quarantined &gt;0.85. False positives: Flagged chunks surfaced to analyst with matched pattern description. Override events logged to Intelligence Oversight Board.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Injection event log</td><td>PostgreSQL (append-only)</td><td>InjectionScreeningEvent per chunk. Risk score, matched patterns, disposition, override events.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Quarantine registry</td><td>Redis</td><td>Active quarantine state per chunk ID. Released by Corpus Quality Specialist review.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Pattern corpus</td><td>Vector store</td><td>Adversarial injection embedding corpus. Updated as Red Team taxonomy evolves.</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:16px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">

  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">MAS / EIDOLON — Media Authenticity Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 5-6</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">

  <div>
   <h5>Risk Thresholds</h5><p>Continuous 0.0–1.0 per artifact per modality. Default: Advisory 0.3–0.6, Flagged 0.6–0.85, Quarantined &gt;0.85. Practice-group-specific overrides via PGS policy. False positives: Flagged artifacts surface to analyst with specific anomaly signals. Override events logged to Intelligence Oversight Board.</p>

    <tr><td>Quarantine registry</td><td>Redis</td><td>Active quarantine state per artifact ID.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Transcript accuracy</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Per-transcript ASR/OCR confidence metadata. Linked to source recording.</td></tr>
   </table>

<div class="layer-header" style="background:#4A2FA8;color:#fff;margin-bottom:10px">Intelligence Layer — themis-intelligence</div>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#4A2FA8;font-size:14px">KCS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">KCS / ARGUS — Knowledge Currency Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">Phase 7-8</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>External Feeds</h5><p>OSINT aggregators, allied intelligence reporting feeds, foreign open-media monitoring, collection system status dashboards, academic and technical publication databases. Feed credentials managed through Vault.</p>
   <h5>Target Watch List</h5><p>Each active requirement registers entities of interest (target organizations, referenced programs, relevant geographies). KCS monitors these specifically and prioritizes invalidation detection for watched entities above general feed monitoring.</p>
   <h5>Polling Intervals</h5><p>OSINT feeds: hourly. Allied reporting: 4–6 hours. News feeds: hourly. Configurable per feed type and matter priority class.</p>
   <h5>Confidence Scoring</h5><p>KCS applies confidence scoring to invalidation events. Low-confidence events (e.g. news reports of a possible ruling before the official docket entry) are surfaced as advisories rather than triggering immediate ActiveInvalidationRequests to TVS.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>ActiveInvalidationRequest Schema</h5><p><code>{source_id, invalidation_type: overruled|amended|superseded|clarified, external_source_uri, effective_date, confidence_score}</code></p>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Feed registry</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Registered external feeds, polling intervals, last successful poll timestamp, feed health status.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Matter watch lists</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Per-matter entity watch list. Active matter entities monitored with priority polling.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Invalidation event log</td><td>PostgreSQL (append-only)</td><td>All ActiveInvalidationRequests published. Immutable audit record of when each source was flagged.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Recent events cache</td><td>Redis (TTL: 24h)</td><td>Recent invalidation events for fast lookup. Intelligence Oversight Board watch list matches.</td></tr>
   </table>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:8px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#4A2FA8;font-size:14px">ERAS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">ERAS / LOGOS — Explainability and Reasoning Audit</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">Phase 7-8</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Reasoning Capture</h5><p>Per-turn structured record: <code>{turn_id, session_id, claims[], reasoning_chain, unsupported_claims[], confidence_signals{}}</code>. Captured asynchronously — does not block response display. Async capture events signed via Vault and published to Kafka → MOIRAI.</p>
   <h5>Unsupported Claim Detection</h5><p>Flags claims where no retrieved evidence in the turn's context window supports the assertion — cross-referenced against the RQS retrieval trace. These PARAM or SYNTH claims with no evidence backing are the highest-priority signal for TCS calibration and FGTS routing.</p>
   <h5>Tradecraft Compliance Surface</h5><p>ERAS reasoning records are the audit artifact for oversight body disclosure. Analysts can produce ERAS records demonstrating: what source intelligence the AI analyzed, what reasoning it applied, what claims it made, and whether those claims were supported by retrieved sources. Structured for dissemination as ICD 203 analytic standards documentation.</p>
   <h5>Latency Target</h5><p>Reasoning capture async (does not block response display). Claim indexing near-real-time in Elasticsearch. Query P95 &lt; 500ms.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Reasoning records</td><td>Elasticsearch</td><td>Per-turn reasoning captures indexed by session, turn, claim, domain. Full-text searchable. Primary query surface for Context Window Inspector and tradecraft compliance export.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Claim index metadata</td><td>PostgreSQL</td><td>Claim provenance links — connecting ERAS claim records to MOIRAI provenance graph nodes. Enables cross-service audit queries.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Event log</td><td>Kafka → MOIRAI</td><td>ReasoningRecord events signed and published. Immutable audit record integrated into provenance graph.</td></tr>
   </table>
   <h5 style="margin-top:10px">ATHENA Integration</h5><p>ERAS reasoning captures surface in: Context Window Inspector (per-exchange reasoning record), Claim Confidence Badges (ERAS unsupported claim signals inform TCS badge scoring), Dissemination Gate (ERAS reasoning record included in every export artifact).</p>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<details class="iv-card" style="margin-bottom:24px">
 <summary style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 16px;cursor:pointer;background:#fff;list-style:none">
  <span class="svc-code" style="color:#4A2FA8;font-size:14px">MDS</span>
  <span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);flex:1">MDS / KRONOS — Model Drift Service</span>
  <span class="badge" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">Phase 3-4</span><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-left:4px">▸</span>
 </summary>
 <div class="iv-body"><div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px">
  <div>
   <h5>Version Polling</h5><p>Polls vendor API version endpoints every 4 hours (configurable per vendor). Compares current vendor-reported model version against last known version. Any change triggers ModelVersionChangeEvent immediately on detection — within 4 hours of vendor release.</p>
   <h5>Active Matter Flagging</h5><p>On version change, queries MOIRAI for all active sessions in the prior 30 days (configurable) that used the prior version. Generates flagged matter list surfaced in Intelligence Oversight Board dashboard. TCS/MIMIR receives re-validation requests — calibration baselines flagged as "potentially stale pending review," not reset or invalidated.</p>
   <h5>Version Pinning</h5><p>Pinned matters route all inference calls to the pinned model version at the Inference Gateway. Pin enforcement is at the gateway level — not the application level. Pin release requires Intelligence Oversight Board acknowledgment for active requirements. Version pinning registry maintained in Redis with PostgreSQL audit log.</p>
  </div>
  <div>
   <h5>Storage</h5>
   <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:0">
    <tr><th>Layer</th><th>Technology</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
    <tr><td>Version history registry</td><td>PostgreSQL (append-only)</td><td>Immutable record: {vendor, model_id, version_string, detected_at, active_matters_affected[]}. Cannot be modified — audit artifact.</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Current version state</td><td>Redis</td><td>Current model version per vendor. Primary comparison point for polling. TTL: 5 hours (refreshed every poll cycle).</td></tr>
    <tr><td>Version pin registry</td><td>Redis + PostgreSQL</td><td>Active version pins per matter. Redis for real-time enforcement at Inference Gateway. PostgreSQL for audit log of pin activations and releases.</td></tr>
   </table>
   <h5 style="margin-top:10px">Governance Surface</h5><p>Intelligence Oversight Board dashboard shows: current model versions per vendor, pending re-validation matters, active version pins, and historical version change log with affected matter counts.</p>
  </div>
 </div></div>
</details>

<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION 12 — EVIDENCE OBJECT MODEL
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<div class="section" id="evidence">
<div class="section-title">Canonical Source Intelligence Object Model</div>
<div class="section-sub">Single canonical representation for all intelligence source types. THEMIS services annotate the object at each stage of the intelligence lifecycle. TypeScript definition file available as evidence-object-model.ts.</div>

<style>
.eo-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:12px;margin-bottom:24px}
.eo-card{border-radius:8px;padding:14px;border:1px solid var(--border)}
.eo-card h4{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px}
.eo-card p{font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55}
.eo-field{font-size:11px;font-family:'SF Mono',Consolas,monospace;padding:3px 7px;background:var(--bg);border-radius:3px;border:1px solid var(--border);display:inline-block;margin:2px}
.eo-code{background:#F4F6FA;color:#1A2A3A;padding:20px 24px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid var(--border);font-family:'SF Mono',Consolas,monospace;font-size:11px;line-height:1.7;overflow:auto;max-height:480px;margin-bottom:16px}
.eo-kw{color:#1E40AF}
.eo-ty{color:#0D6B5E}
.eo-str{color:#9A6800}
.eo-cm{color:#5A6480;font-style:italic}
.eo-section{font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.12em;color:var(--muted);margin:16px 0 8px;padding-bottom:4px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
</style>

<!-- Design decisions -->
<div class="grid grid-2" style="margin-bottom:24px;gap:16px">
 <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #1A9E8C">
  <h4 style="color:#0D6B5E">Four design decisions</h4>
  <ul style="margin-top:8px;padding-left:16px;font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.7">
   <li><strong>Classification is first-class</strong> — not a metadata field. Every source intelligence object carries a PCES/AEGIS-validated compartment classification. For COMMUNICATION intercepts, classification is a function of participant roles and collection authority.</li>
   <li><strong>Derivative artifacts are explicit</strong> — TRANSCRIPT carries <code>is_derivative: true</code> and a reference to the source recording. It is never GRND-equivalent.</li>
   <li><strong>Authenticity and adversarial screening are orthogonal</strong> — MAS asks "is this artifact genuine?". IAS asks "has this artifact been weaponized?". Both annotate the object independently.</li>
   <li><strong>Lifecycle state drives ATHENA badge behavior</strong> — QUARANTINED evidence cannot appear as a retrieval source. FLAGGED surfaces a warning. The lifecycle state machine is the bridge between THEMIS services and the analyst interface.</li>
  </ul>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #1B3A5C">
  <h4 style="color:#1B3A5C">THEMIS service annotation map</h4>
  <table class="decision-table" style="margin-top:8px">
   <tr><th>Service</th><th>Writes to</th><th>Trigger</th></tr>
   <tr><td>PCES/AEGIS</td><td><code>classification.*</code></td><td>Every ingestion</td></tr>
   <tr><td>MAS/EIDOLON</td><td><code>authenticity.*</code></td><td>Media ingestion</td></tr>
   <tr><td>IAS/SCUDO</td><td><code>adversarial.*</code></td><td>Chunk ingestion</td></tr>
   <tr><td>TVS/KAIROS</td><td><code>validity.*</code></td><td>Ingestion + KCS invalidation</td></tr>
   <tr><td>CVS/VERITAS</td><td><code>citations.*</code></td><td>Generation-time (not ingestion)</td></tr>
   <tr><td>RQS/HERMES</td><td><code>retrieval.rqs_quality_score</code></td><td>Post-retrieval scoring</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Provenance/MOIRAI</td><td><code>provenance.*</code></td><td>All lifecycle events</td></tr>
   <tr><td>TCS/MIMIR</td><td>Reads validity + authenticity scores to set confidence ceilings</td><td>Claim scoring</td></tr>
   <tr><td>FGTS/ALETHEIA</td><td>Reads validity + authenticity to weight corrections citing this evidence</td><td>Verification outcomes</td></tr>
  </table>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- Evidence type taxonomy -->
<div class="eo-section">Evidence Type Taxonomy</div>
<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:10px;margin-bottom:24px">
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #2E5FAD"><h4 style="color:#2E5FAD">DOCUMENT</h4><p>PDFs, Word docs, text files. GRND / SYNTH / PARAM badges. CVS/VERITAS source corroboration applies.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">modality: text</span></div></div>
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #6D4FB5"><h4 style="color:#6D4FB5">AUDIO</h4><p>Recordings, source debriefings, voicemails. AUDIO badge. MAS/EIDOLON screening. TRANSCRIPT derivative expected.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">modality: audio</span></div></div>
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #4A2FA8"><h4 style="color:#4A2FA8">VIDEO</h4><p>Recordings, surveillance, source debriefings. VIDEO badge. MAS deepfake and temporal screening. TRANSCRIPT derivative expected.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">modality: video</span></div></div>
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #8B2222"><h4 style="color:#8B2222">IMAGE</h4><p>Photographs, screenshots, exhibits. IMAGE badge. MAS GAN/EXIF/steganography screening.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">modality: image</span></div></div>
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #7A5000"><h4 style="color:#7A5000">SCANNED_DOCUMENT</h4><p>OCR-processed scans. OCR badge with inline confidence score. Per-region confidence from MAS/EIDOLON creates hard TCS confidence ceiling.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">source: image</span><span class="eo-field">resolved: text</span></div></div>
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #0D6B5E"><h4 style="color:#0D6B5E">TRANSCRIPT</h4><p>Derivative of AUDIO or VIDEO. <strong>Never GRND-equivalent.</strong> is_derivative: true is structural. ASR accuracy profile is the binding confidence constraint.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">is_derivative: true</span></div></div>
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #1B3A5C"><h4 style="color:#1B3A5C">COMMUNICATION</h4><p>Email, chat, SMS. Own content extension. Participant roles (sender/to/cc/bcc, is_analyst, is_client) are first-class — they drive compartment classification.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">modality: text</span></div></div>
 <div class="eo-card" style="border-top:4px solid #9A6800"><h4 style="color:#9A6800">COURT_FILING / REGULATORY_FILING</h4><p>Extends DOCUMENT. KCS/ARGUS monitors docket/regulatory feeds. docket_entry_id and public_record_id support watch list matching.</p><div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="eo-field">kcs_watched</span></div></div>
</div>

<!-- Lifecycle state machine -->
<div class="eo-section">Lifecycle State Machine</div>
<div style="background:#F4F7FB;border-radius:8px;padding:16px 20px;margin-bottom:24px">
 <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center">
  <div style="background:#9A6800;color:#fff;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">PENDING_SCREENING</div>
  <span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:14px">→</span>
  <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px">
   <div style="background:#1A9E8C;color:#fff;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">ACTIVE</div>
   <div style="background:#B87800;color:#fff;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">ADVISORY</div>
   <div style="background:#B84040;color:#fff;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">FLAGGED</div>
   <div style="background:#1B3A5C;color:#fff;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">QUARANTINED</div>
  </div>
  <span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:14px">→</span>
  <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px">
   <div style="background:#4A5568;color:#fff;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">ARCHIVED</div>
   <div style="background:#4A5568;color:#fff;padding:5px 10px;border-radius:5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">DELETED</div>
  </div>
 </div>
 <div style="margin-top:12px;display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:8px">
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)"><strong>QUARANTINED ← ACTIVE/ADVISORY/FLAGGED</strong> — on re-screening escalation or Intelligence Oversight Board decision</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)"><strong>QUARANTINED → ACTIVE/ADVISORY/FLAGGED</strong> — Corpus Quality Specialist release with documented reason</div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- Object structure -->
<div class="eo-section">Base Object Structure</div>
<div class="eo-code">
<span class="eo-kw">interface</span> <span class="eo-ty">EvidenceObject</span> {<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// IDENTITY</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;evidence_id:    <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;matter_id:      <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;evidence_type:  <span class="eo-ty">EvidenceType</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;modality:       <span class="eo-ty">Modality</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;content_hash:   <span class="eo-ty">SHA256</span>; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// SHA-256 of raw artifact at ingestion — tamper detection</span><br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// SOURCE PROVENANCE</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;source:         <span class="eo-ty">EvidenceSource</span>; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// custody_chain: CustodyEntry[] append-only</span><br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// PRIVILEGE — first-class, not metadata. PCES/AEGIS-validated at ingestion.</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;privilege:      <span class="eo-ty">ClassificationRecord</span>;<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// THEMIS SERVICE ANNOTATIONS</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;authenticity:   <span class="eo-ty">AuthenticityRecord</span>;  &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// MAS/EIDOLON — is this artifact genuine?</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;adversarial:    <span class="eo-ty">AdversarialRecord</span>;   &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// IAS/SCUDO — has this artifact been weaponized?</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;validity:       <span class="eo-ty">ValidityRecord</span>;      &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// TVS/KAIROS — is this still current?</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;citations:      <span class="eo-ty">CitationRecord</span> | <span class="eo-kw">null</span>;   <span class="eo-cm">// CVS/VERITAS — at generation time, not ingestion</span><br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// CONTENT — type-specific extension (use type guards per evidence_type)</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;content:        <span class="eo-ty">EvidenceContent</span>;<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// RETRIEVAL</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;retrieval:      <span class="eo-ty">RetrievalRecord</span>; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// MIMIR + RQS/HERMES</span><br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// PROVENANCE GRAPH — MOIRAI</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;provenance:     <span class="eo-ty">ProvenanceRecord</span>; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// moirai_node_id + signing_key_id + prev_event_hash</span><br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// RELATIONSHIPS</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;relations: {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parent_id:     <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span> | <span class="eo-kw">null</span>;     <span class="eo-cm">// If this is a derivative (e.g. transcript of recording)</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;derived_ids:   <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span>[];          <span class="eo-cm">// Derivatives produced from this</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;version_chain: <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span>[];          <span class="eo-cm">// SCRIBE version history — oldest first</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;superseded_by: <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span> | <span class="eo-kw">null</span>;     <span class="eo-cm">// If a later version is now authoritative</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;};<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// LIFECYCLE</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;lifecycle:      <span class="eo-ty">LifecycleRecord</span>; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// state + state_history append-only</span><br>
<br>
}
</div>

<!-- Communication extension -->
<div class="eo-section">COMMUNICATION Content Extension — Participant Roles Drive Privilege</div>
<div class="eo-code">
<span class="eo-kw">interface</span> <span class="eo-ty">CommunicationContent</span> <span class="eo-kw">extends</span> <span class="eo-ty">EvidenceContent</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;modality:    <span class="eo-str">'text'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;platform:    <span class="eo-ty">CommunicationPlatform</span>; &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// email | teams | slack | whatsapp | sms | imessage | signal | zoom_chat | ...</span><br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Thread structure</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;thread_id:        <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;message_id:       <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;         <span class="eo-cm">// Platform-canonical message identifier</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;in_reply_to:      <span class="eo-ty">string</span> | <span class="eo-kw">null</span>;   <span class="eo-cm">// Parent message_id within thread</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;thread_position:  <span class="eo-ty">number</span>;         <span class="eo-cm">// 0-indexed chronological position in thread</span><br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Subject (email and some platforms)</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;subject:     <span class="eo-ty">string</span> | <span class="eo-kw">null</span>;<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Participants — roles are first-class because they determine privilege</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;sender:      <span class="eo-ty">CommunicationParticipant</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;to:          <span class="eo-ty">CommunicationParticipant</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;cc:          <span class="eo-ty">CommunicationParticipant</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;bcc:         <span class="eo-ty">CommunicationParticipant</span>[];<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Body</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;body:        <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;body_format: <span class="eo-str">'plain_text'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'html'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'rich_text'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;word_count:  <span class="eo-ty">number</span>;<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Attachments — each is a separate evidence object</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;attachment_ids: <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span>[];<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Timestamps</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;sent_at:     <span class="eo-ty">Timestamp</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;received_at: <span class="eo-ty">Timestamp</span> | <span class="eo-kw">null</span>;<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Message state — important for forensic recovery</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;is_edited:             <span class="eo-ty">boolean</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;edited_at:             <span class="eo-ty">Timestamp</span> | <span class="eo-kw">null</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;is_recovered_deletion: <span class="eo-ty">boolean</span>;  <span class="eo-cm">// True if deleted but forensically recovered</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;deletion_timestamp?:   <span class="eo-ty">Timestamp</span>;<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Platform-specific metadata (email headers, delivery receipts, etc.)</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;platform_metadata:     <span class="eo-ty">Record</span>&lt;<span class="eo-ty">string</span>, <span class="eo-ty">unknown</span>&gt;;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;thread_message_ids:    <span class="eo-ty">string</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;legal_citations:       <span class="eo-ty">LegalCitation</span>[];<br>
}<br>
<br>
<span class="eo-cm">// is_analyst and is_client are populated by PCES/AEGIS at ingestion</span><br>
<span class="eo-cm">// by cross-referencing the agency's analyst roster and cleared personnel registry.</span><br>
<span class="eo-cm">// These fields DRIVE compartment classification — they do not describe it.</span><br>
<span class="eo-kw">interface</span> <span class="eo-ty">CommunicationParticipant</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;display_name:     <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;address:          <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;       <span class="eo-cm">// Email address, phone, or platform handle</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;platform_user_id?: <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;is_analyst:      <span class="eo-ty">boolean</span>;      <span class="eo-cm">// PCES cross-ref: firm analyst roster</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;is_client:        <span class="eo-ty">boolean</span>;      <span class="eo-cm">// PCES cross-ref: firm cleared personnel registry</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;organization?:    <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;participant_role: <span class="eo-str">'sender'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'to'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'cc'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'bcc'</span>;<br>
}
</div>

<!-- TRANSCRIPT extension callout -->
<div class="eo-section">TRANSCRIPT Content Extension — Derivative Artifact Rules</div>
<div style="background:#E6F5F3;border:1px solid #A0D0C4;border-radius:8px;padding:14px 18px;margin-bottom:16px">
 <h4 style="color:#0D6B5E;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:6px">TRANSCRIPT is never GRND-equivalent</h4>
 <p style="font-size:12px;color:#0A4A40;line-height:1.65">A transcript is a model-generated artifact with its own error rate independent of the source recording's authenticity. <code>is_derivative: true</code> is a structural marker — it cannot be false for the TRANSCRIPT type. The <code>source_evidence_id</code> reference to the parent AUDIO or VIDEO object is required. The <code>overall_accuracy</code> and <code>low_confidence_segments</code> fields are the binding constraint on TCS/MIMIR confidence ceilings for claims sourced from this transcript — TCS cannot assign HIGH confidence to a claim from a low-confidence transcript region regardless of model self-reported confidence.</p>
</div>
<div class="eo-code">
<span class="eo-kw">interface</span> <span class="eo-ty">TranscriptContent</span> <span class="eo-kw">extends</span> <span class="eo-ty">EvidenceContent</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;modality:                 <span class="eo-str">'text'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;is_derivative:            <span class="eo-kw">true</span>;      <span class="eo-cm">// Structural — cannot be false</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;source_evidence_id:       <span class="eo-ty">UUID</span>;      <span class="eo-cm">// The AUDIO or VIDEO this was derived from</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;source_evidence_type:     <span class="eo-str">'AUDIO'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'VIDEO'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;transcription_engine:     <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;transcription_method:     <span class="eo-str">'automated'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'human'</span> | <span class="eo-str">'hybrid'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;asr_model_version?:       <span class="eo-ty">string</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;overall_accuracy?:        <span class="eo-ty">number</span>;    <span class="eo-cm">// 0.0 – 1.0; null for human transcriptions</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;duration_seconds:         <span class="eo-ty">number</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;word_count:               <span class="eo-ty">number</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;speakers:                 <span class="eo-ty">SpeakerSegment</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;low_confidence_segments:  <span class="eo-ty">TranscriptSegment</span>[];  <span class="eo-cm">// Surfaces in ATHENA TRANSCRIPT badge</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;reviewed_by?:             <span class="eo-ty">AnalystID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;review_timestamp?:        <span class="eo-ty">Timestamp</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;legal_citations:          <span class="eo-ty">LegalCitation</span>[];<br>
}
</div>

<!-- Source type badge mapping -->
<div class="eo-section">Source Type Badge Mapping Function</div>
<div class="eo-code">
<span class="eo-cm">/**<br>
&nbsp;* Returns the ATHENA source type badge for claims derived from this evidence.<br>
&nbsp;* Canonical mapping from evidence type + state to badge type.<br>
&nbsp;*/</span><br>
<span class="eo-kw">function</span> <span class="eo-ty">sourceTypeBadge</span>(e: <span class="eo-ty">EvidenceObject</span>): <span class="eo-ty">string</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-kw">if</span> (e.evidence_type === <span class="eo-str">'TRANSCRIPT'</span>)       <span class="eo-kw">return</span> <span class="eo-str">'TRANSCRIPT'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-kw">if</span> (e.evidence_type === <span class="eo-str">'VIDEO'</span>)            <span class="eo-kw">return</span> <span class="eo-str">'VIDEO'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-kw">if</span> (e.evidence_type === <span class="eo-str">'AUDIO'</span>)            <span class="eo-kw">return</span> <span class="eo-str">'AUDIO'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-kw">if</span> (e.evidence_type === <span class="eo-str">'IMAGE'</span>)            <span class="eo-kw">return</span> <span class="eo-str">'IMAGE'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-kw">if</span> (e.evidence_type === <span class="eo-str">'SCANNED_DOCUMENT'</span>) <span class="eo-kw">return</span> <span class="eo-str">`OCR ${</span><span class="eo-ty">Math</span>.round(<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;(e.authenticity.modality_scores?.ocr?.overall ?? <span class="eo-str">1</span>) * <span class="eo-str">100</span>)<span class="eo-str">}%`</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-cm">// Text-bearing types: GRND if retrieved, SYNTH if synthesised, PARAM if parametric</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="eo-kw">return</span> <span class="eo-str">'GRND'</span>;<br>
}
</div>

</div>
