<!-- Three-problem overview -->
<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr);gap:10px;margin-bottom:28px">
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #8B2222;padding:12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#8B2222;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">Problem 1 · Retroactive</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:5px;font-size:13px">Derived Information Persistence</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55">What happens to AI outputs when source evidence access is subsequently revoked.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="ac-phase" style="background:#FBF0F0;color:#8B2222">Phase 3-4</span></div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #9A6800;padding:12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#9A6800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">Problem 2 · Prospective</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:5px;font-size:13px">Retrieval Coverage Gaps</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55">Surfacing the existence of relevant inaccessible evidence without revealing its content.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="ac-phase" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 5-6</span></div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1B3A5C;padding:12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">Problem 3 · Preventive</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:5px;font-size:13px">Query-Layer Access Control</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55">Queries as privileged analytic work product, cross-matter contamination, query-type authorization.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="ac-phase" style="background:#EEF2F8;color:#1B3A5C">1-2 + 3-4</span></div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #0D6B5E;padding:12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#0D6B5E;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">Problem 4 · Structural</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:5px;font-size:13px">Within-Matter Access Granularity</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55">Protective order tiers, role-based access within matters, classification log generation.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="ac-phase" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">1-2 + 3-4</span></div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #4A2FA8;padding:12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#4A2FA8;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">Problem 5 · Temporal</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:5px;font-size:13px">Temporal and Situational Access</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55">Time-limited grants, break-glass emergency access, historical access reconstruction.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:6px"><span class="ac-phase" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">Phase 3-4</span></div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- ── PROBLEM 1 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec">Problem 1 — Derived Information Persistence</h3>

<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:16px">
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">AccessRevocationEvent — new event type</h4>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:8px">First-class signed event produced by PCES/AEGIS when any access control decision changes in a way that reduces access to a previously accessible evidence object. All downstream services consume via existing Kafka consumer groups.</p>
  <div class="ac-schema">
<span class="kw">interface</span> <span class="ty">AccessRevocationEvent</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;evidence_id:          <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;matter_id:            <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;revocation_reason:    <span class="ty">RevocationReason</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;revocation_type:      <span class="st">'full'</span> | <span class="st">'analyst_scoped'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;affected_analyst_ids?: <span class="ty">AnalystID</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;effective_at:         <span class="ty">Timestamp</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;signed:               <span class="kw">true</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;prev_event_hash:      <span class="ty">SHA256</span>;<br>
}<br><br>
<span class="cm">// RevocationReason:</span><br>
<span class="cm">// classification_change</span><br>
<span class="cm">// matter_scope_change</span><br>
<span class="cm">// conflict_of_interest</span><br>
<span class="cm">// inadvertent_disclosure_recovery</span><br>
<span class="cm">// administrative</span>
  </div>
 </div>
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">Event-driven blast radius propagation</h4>
  <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px">
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #1B3A5C;padding:10px 14px">
    <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;margin-bottom:4px">PCES/AEGIS → produces AccessRevocationEvent</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub)">Fires on any classification, requirement scope, or CoI change reducing access. Signed and published to Kafka.</div>
   </div>
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #0D6B5E;padding:10px 14px">
    <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#0D6B5E;margin-bottom:4px">MOIRAI → reverse DAG traversal</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub)">Walks the provenance graph backward from the revoked evidence. Enumerates all derived sessions, turns, claims, documents, reasoning records. Emits RevocationBlastRadiusEvent. SLA: 60 minutes.</div>
   </div>
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #0D6B5E;padding:10px 14px">
    <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#0D6B5E;margin-bottom:4px">DPS/CODEX → document marking + dissemination gate</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub)">Marks affected documents <code>review_required: true</code>. Highlights affected paragraphs in the provenance panel. Dissemination gate hard-blocks until supervisory acknowledgment is logged to MOIRAI.</div>
   </div>
   <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #4A2FA8;padding:10px 14px">
    <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#4A2FA8;margin-bottom:4px">ERAS/LOGOS → reasoning record marking</div>
    <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub)">Marks affected reasoning records. Revocation indicator shown in session view. Professional responsibility export requires supervisory acknowledgment.</div>
   </div>
  </div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- Blast radius registry table -->
<h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">Blast Radius Registry — MOIRAI (PostgreSQL, append-only)</h4>
<table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:16px">
 <tr><th>Field</th><th>Type</th><th>Purpose</th></tr>
 <tr><td><code>revocation_event_id</code></td><td>UUID</td><td>Source AccessRevocationEvent reference</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>artifact_type</code></td><td>string</td><td>session / turn / claim / document / reasoning_record</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>artifact_id</code></td><td>UUID</td><td>Affected artifact identifier</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>remediation_status</code></td><td>enum</td><td>pending → reviewed → acknowledged → escalated</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>reviewed_by</code></td><td>AnalystID</td><td>Supervising analyst who reviewed</td></tr>
 <tr><td><code>reviewed_at</code></td><td>Timestamp</td><td>Review completion timestamp</td></tr>
</table>

<!-- Revocation notice callout -->
<div class="ac-notice" style="background:#FBF0F0;border:1px solid #E8B4B4">
 <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#8B2222;margin-bottom:6px">ATHENA — Revocation Notice (blocking for dissemination)</div>
 <div style="font-size:12px;color:#6B1818;font-style:italic;margin-bottom:8px">"Evidence used in this session is no longer accessible to you. [N] claims and [M] document paragraphs in this matter may be affected. A supervising analyst must review before this analytic work product is disseminated or filed."</div>
 <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#6B1818">Blocking for dissemination until supervisor acknowledgment · Informational only for continued session work · Supervisor receives parallel blast radius summary</div>
</div>

<div class="ac-notice" style="background:#FFF7E8;border:1px solid #E0C880;margin-top:8px">
 <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#9A6800;margin-bottom:4px">Professional Responsibility Boundary</div>
 <div style="font-size:12px;color:#7A5000;line-height:1.6">THEMIS enumerates and enforces. It cannot remediate the fact that synthesis was read prior to revocation. Remediation procedures for IC oversight framework Rule 5 inadvertent disclosure recovery scenarios require General Counsel / Inspector General input. See <strong>GC-1</strong> below.</div>
</div>

<!-- ── PROBLEM 2 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec" style="margin-top:32px">Problem 2 — Retrieval Coverage Gaps</h3>

<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:16px">
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">The Privileged MIMIR Channel</h4>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:8px">A second retrieval pass against the full corpus, bypassing PCES access scope filtering, for relevance comparison only. Returns scores and IDs — never content. Never enters the synthesis path.</p>
  <div class="ac-constraint">
   <h5>6 Constraints — All Enforced Before Phase 5-6</h5>
   <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#6B1818;line-height:1.7">
    1. Returns relevance scores and evidence_ids only — never content<br>
    2. Output never enters the context window for synthesis<br>
    3. Accessible only to the RQS gap detection function<br>
    4. Every query logged to MOIRAI: <code>purpose: gap_detection_only</code><br>
    5. Intelligence Oversight Board formal approval required<br>
    6. Dedicated security architecture review required
   </div>
  </div>
 </div>
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">RQS/HERMES — Gap Coverage Metric</h4>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:8px">New retrieval quality dimension alongside precision, miss rate, and embedding drift.</p>
  <div class="ac-schema">
<span class="cm">// Gap Coverage Score</span><br>
gap_score =<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;Σ(relevance of <span class="st">excluded</span> chunks above threshold)<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;÷ Σ(relevance of <span class="st">all</span> chunks above threshold)<br><br>
<span class="cm">// 0.0 = all relevant evidence in scope</span><br>
<span class="cm">// 0.4 = ~40% of relevant weight excluded</span><br><br>
<span class="cm">// Threshold: configurable by PGS/NOMOS</span><br>
<span class="cm">// Default: fire indicator when gap_score > 0.25</span>
  </div>
  <div class="ac-schema">
<span class="kw">interface</span> <span class="ty">RelevanceGapEvent</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;session_id:              <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;query_id:                <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;gap_score:               <span class="ty">number</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;excluded_evidence_count: <span class="ty">number</span>; <span class="cm">// count only</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;threshold_exceeded:      <span class="ty">boolean</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="cm">// excluded_evidence_ids stored in RQS</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="cm">// gap log — never in this event</span><br>
}
  </div>
 </div>
</div>

<div class="ac-notice" style="background:#FFF7E8;border:1px solid #E0C880;margin-bottom:16px">
 <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#9A6800;margin-bottom:6px">ATHENA — Gap Indicator</div>
 <div style="font-size:12px;color:#7A5000;font-style:italic;margin-bottom:8px">"Additional evidence relevant to this query exists in this matter that you do not currently have access to. A supervising analyst should review this analysis before it is used in analytic work product."</div>
 <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:#7A5000">Shows: gap level (Low / Moderate / High) · One-click "Flag for Supervisor Review" escalation<br>Never shows: what the excluded evidence is, how many items, or any characterizing information about its content</div>
</div>

<div style="background:#E6F5F3;border:1px solid #A0D0C4;border-radius:6px;padding:12px 16px;font-size:12px;color:#0A4A40;line-height:1.6">
 <strong>Escalation path:</strong> "Flag for Supervisor Review" creates a SupervisorReviewRequest in DPS/CODEX surfaced in the supervising analyst's ATHENA dashboard. The supervisor — with full matter access — reviews the analysis in context of the complete evidence set and provides an acknowledgment or revised finding. All escalation events logged to MOIRAI as signed events.
</div>

<!-- ── PROBLEM 3 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec" style="margin-top:32px">Problem 3 — Query-Layer Access Control</h3>

<div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #1B3A5C;margin-bottom:16px;padding:12px 16px">
 <h4 style="color:#1B3A5C;margin-bottom:8px">Why queries require access control</h4>
 <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65">Queries are not neutral. <em>"What evidence supports our client's alibi for March 5th?"</em> reveals collection strategy, theory of the case, and the existence of a specific matter concern. <em>"What does the confidential settlement agreement say about the non-disclosure scope?"</em> reveals the existence of a specific settlement. Queries are analyst analytic work product and must be governed from the first analyst interaction.</p>
</div>

<div class="grid grid-3" style="gap:12px;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #1B3A5C">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">3a · Phase 1-2</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:6px">Query Privilege Classification</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px"><strong>PGS/NOMOS</strong> classifies every query as analyst analytic work product at ingestion by default, before any processing. Intelligence Oversight Board may override for specific administrative interaction classes.</p>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55"><strong>MOIRAI</strong> query records gain a <code>query_privilege</code> field. ANALYTIC_WORK_PRODUCT queries are subject to the same requirement scope access control as evidence objects.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:8px;background:#FBF0F0;border-radius:4px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;color:#6B1818">Highest-priority enhancement. Must be Phase 1-2. Queries must be analytic work product from the first analyst interaction.</div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #9A6800">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#9A6800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">3b · Phase 3-4</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:6px">Cross-Matter Contamination</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px"><strong>PCES/AEGIS</strong> gains a cross-matter entity detection function using a matter entity registry (parties, case references, key terms per matter). Emits CrossMatterContaminationSignal on detection.</p>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55"><strong>IAS/SCUDO</strong> adds a privilege contamination classifier alongside the adversarial injection classifiers — same pipeline position, distinct detection path using matter-specific entity embeddings.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:8px;background:#FFF7E8;border-radius:4px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;color:#7A5000">Advisory default. Query proceeds with signal logged to MOIRAI. PGS/NOMOS can configure as blocking for high-sensitivity matters.</div>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #4A2FA8">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#4A2FA8;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">3c · Phase 3-4</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:6px">Query-Type Authorization</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px"><strong>PGS/NOMOS</strong> adds a query-type authorization policy dimension. A lightweight classifier assigns a query_type label evaluated against the authorization policy for the current interaction class.</p>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55">Queries requiring supervisory authorization are queued. The supervisor receives the unprocessed query text and approves or rejects. All authorization events are signed MOIRAI events.</p>
  <div style="margin-top:8px;background:#EEEBFF;border-radius:4px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:11px;color:#4A2FA8">Taxonomy requires Intelligence Oversight Board determination. See GC-3.</div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- Query-type authorization table -->
<h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">Default Query-Type Authorization Taxonomy</h4>
<table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:20px">
 <tr><th>Query Type</th><th>Default Authorization</th></tr>
 <tr><td>Collection exposure or target sensitivity assessment</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Supervisory acknowledgment required</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Adversary strategy or likely intent</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Supervisory acknowledgment required</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Firm internal liability exposure assessment</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Supervisory acknowledgment required</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Privilege waiver risk assessment</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Supervisory acknowledgment required</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Witness credibility or impeachment strategy</td><td><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:600">Standard — no additional authorization</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Intelligence research and source analysis</td><td><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:600">Standard — no additional authorization</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Evidence characterization and summary</td><td><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:600">Standard — no additional authorization</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Document comparison and version analysis</td><td><span style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:600">Standard — no additional authorization</span></td></tr>
</table>

<!-- ── SEQUENCING ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<!-- ── PROBLEM 4 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec" style="margin-top:32px">Problem 4 — Within-Matter Access Granularity</h3>

<div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #0D6B5E;padding:12px 16px;margin-bottom:16px">
 <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65;margin:0">Current PCES/AEGIS enforces binary matter-scoped access. Real matters have internal tiers: courts issue protective orders that classify documents as Confidential, Highly Confidential — TS//SCI — Analysts' Eyes Only, or Highly Confidential — Source Code. A intelligence analyst (junior) may work on a matter but cannot access AEO-designated documents. An outside expert may access a specific evidence subset but nothing else. The existing model has no mechanism for these distinctions.</p>
</div>

<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:16px">
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">4a — Protective Order Tier Model · PCES/AEGIS</h4>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:8px">PCES/AEGIS moves from binary matter-scoped access to a multi-dimensional check. Both the evidence object and the analyst's access record carry a tier designation. Tier is tagged at evidence ingestion. Tier changes (oversight directive modification) produce <code>AccessRevocationEvent</code> via the Problem 1 machinery — tier enforcement connects to derived information propagation automatically.</p>
  <div class="ac-schema">
<span class="kw">type</span> <span class="ty">ProtectiveOrderTier</span> =<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;| <span class="st">'UNCLASSIFIED'</span>    <span class="cm">// No protective order</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;| <span class="st">'CONFIDENTIAL'</span>    <span class="cm">// Full analytic team</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;| <span class="st">'TS_SCI_AEO'</span>         <span class="cm">// TS//SCI — Analysts' Eyes Only</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;| <span class="st">'TS_SCI_SAP'</span> <span class="cm">// Designated analysts only</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;| <span class="st">'SAP_ONLY'</span>      <span class="cm">// Court review only</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;| <span class="st">'SEALED'</span>;<br><br>
<span class="cm">// New multi-dimensional access check:</span><br>
access(analyst, evidence) =<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;matter_scope_check()<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kw">AND</span> privilege_check()<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kw">AND</span> tier_check(analyst.tier_grants, evidence.tier)<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="kw">AND</span> role_check(analyst.matter_role, evidence.tier)
  </div>
  <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:8px">Phase 1-2. Tier must be tagged at ingestion from day one — retroactive re-classification is operationally expensive.</p>
 </div>
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">Role-Tier Access Matrix</h4>
  <table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:12px">
   <tr><th>Role</th><th>CONF</th><th>TS_SCI_AEO</th><th>HC_SRC</th><th>IN_CAM</th></tr>
   <tr><td>Supervising Partner</td><td style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700">✓</td><td style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700">✓</td><td>By desig.</td><td>By order</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Associate Analyst</td><td style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700">✓</td><td style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700">✓</td><td>By desig.</td><td>By order</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Intelligence Analyst (Junior)</td><td style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700">✓</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Collection Specialist</td><td style="color:#1A9E8C;font-weight:700">✓</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Outside Expert</td><td>By grant</td><td>By grant</td><td>By grant</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Client</td><td>By grant</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">✗</td></tr>
  </table>
  <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:12px">Default matrix. Firm-specific role definitions require General Counsel / Inspector General and HR input before Phase 1-2 deployment. See GC-4.</p>
  <h4 style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">PGS/NOMOS — Tier-Aware Policy</h4>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6">Interaction class policies gain a tier awareness dimension. Allowed response types, verification requirements, and dissemination gate thresholds are configurable by protective order tier. A session retrieving TS_SCI_AEO documents may carry stricter export constraints than one operating entirely on CONFIDENTIAL material. Policy rules referencing tier require Intelligence Oversight Board approval.</p>
 </div>
</div>

<div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #9A6800;padding:12px 16px;margin-bottom:8px">
 <h4 style="color:#9A6800;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:6px">4b — Classification Log Generation · PCES/AEGIS · Phase 5-6</h4>
 <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:8px">A formal classification log is a discovery obligation. PCES/AEGIS already holds everything needed: the privilege record per evidence object plus the COMMUNICATION content extension's participant roles. The classification log generator queries PCES + MOIRAI for a given matter, filters to withheld or redacted-in-part documents, and produces a structured log.</p>
 <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">
  <div><span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted)">Document ID / Bates</span><br><span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)">evidence_id + matter registry</span></div>
  <div><span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted)">Type and date</span><br><span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)">content extension fields</span></div>
  <div><span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted)">Author and recipients</span><br><span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)">COMMUNICATION participant roles</span></div>
  <div><span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted)">Privilege basis</span><br><span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)">classification.classification</span></div>
  <div><span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:var(--muted)">Withheld / redacted</span><br><span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)">matter production record</span></div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- ── PROBLEM 5 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec" style="margin-top:32px">Problem 5 — Temporal and Situational Access</h3>

<div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #4A2FA8;padding:12px 16px;margin-bottom:16px">
 <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65;margin:0">Access control is currently static — a point-in-time state. Three temporal dimensions are unaddressed: access rights that are inherently time-bounded, emergency access situations where normal controls would cause operational failure, and the ability to reconstruct exactly what any analyst had access to at any historical moment. All three have tradecraft compliance implications.</p>
</div>

<div class="grid grid-3" style="gap:12px;margin-bottom:16px">

 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #4A2FA8">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#4A2FA8;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">5a · Phase 3-4</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:6px">Time-Limited Access Grants</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px">Access records gain <code>expires_at</code> and <code>grant_type</code>. On expiration, PCES automatically produces an <code>AccessRevocationEvent</code> — expiration feeds directly into Problem 1's blast radius enumeration and DPS/ERAS marking machinery.</p>
  <div class="ac-schema" style="font-size:10px">
grant_type:  <span class="st">'permanent'</span><br>
           | <span class="st">'time_limited'</span><br>
           | <span class="st">'break_glass'</span><br>
expires_at:  <span class="ty">Timestamp</span> | <span class="kw">null</span><br>
granted_for: <span class="ty">string</span> <span class="cm">// Required reason</span><br>
granted_by:  <span class="ty">AnalystID</span>
  </div>
  <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub);margin-top:6px">Use cases: outside expert engagements, co-counsel joining late, client review windows, seconded analysts. Notifications at 24h and 1h before expiry. Explicit renewal required — never auto-renews.</p>
 </div>

 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #8B2222">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#8B2222;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">5b · Phase 3-4</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:6px">Break-Glass Emergency Access</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px">Without a formal procedure, analysts create informal workarounds in urgent situations — which is more dangerous than controlled emergency access. Requires documented justification, supervisor notification, automatic expiration, and mandatory post-hoc review.</p>
  <table class="decision-table" style="font-size:10.5px;margin-bottom:8px">
   <tr><th>Urgency</th><th>Window</th><th>No response</th></tr>
   <tr><td>Standard</td><td>30 min</td><td>Escalate</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Urgent</td><td>15 min</td><td>Escalate</td></tr>
   <tr><td>Critical</td><td>5 min</td><td>Auto-approve + full log</td></tr>
  </table>
  <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub)">Max grant: 24h. Post-hoc review required within 5 business days. Reported to Intelligence Oversight Board monthly. ATHENA shows orange banner: <em>"Break-glass access active — [time remaining]."</em></p>
 </div>

 <div class="card" style="border-top:4px solid #6B7590">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#6B7590;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:5px">5c · Phase 3-4</div>
  <h4 style="color:var(--navy);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:6px">Historical Access Reconstruction</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px">MOIRAI captures what was done. Historical access reconstruction answers a different question: what was a specific analyst authorised to access at a specific historical moment — including evidence they could have accessed but didn't.</p>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px">PCES/AEGIS gains an append-only <strong>AccessStateLog</strong>. Every access change produces an <code>AccessStateChangeEvent</code>.</p>
  <div class="ac-schema" style="font-size:10px">
PCES.getAccessStateAt(<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;analyst_id,<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;matter_id,<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;timestamp<br>
) → <span class="ty">AccessScope</span>
  </div>
  <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--sub);margin-top:6px">Use cases: oversight inquiries, classification downgrade analysis, oversight inquiry defense, conflict retrospectives. Cross-referenced with MOIRAI provenance for the complete picture.</p>
 </div>
</div>

<div class="ac-notice" style="background:#EEEBFF;border:1px solid #C4B5FD;margin-bottom:8px">
 <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#4A2FA8;margin-bottom:4px">Problems 5a and 1 are architecturally connected</div>
 <p style="font-size:12px;color:#3A2080;line-height:1.6;margin:0">Time-limited access expiration (5a) and break-glass expiration (5b) both produce <code>AccessRevocationEvent</code> — the same event that drives Problem 1's blast radius enumeration, DPS document marking, and ERAS reasoning record marking. Designing these in Phase 3-4 together is the correct sequencing. The temporal access machinery is not a parallel system — it feeds the existing derived information propagation pipeline.</p>
</div>

<h3 class="subsec">Implementation Sequencing</h3>
<table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:28px">
 <tr><th>Enhancement</th><th>Depends on</th><th>Phase</th></tr>

 <tr><td><strong>Query compartment classification — 3a</strong></td><td>PGS/NOMOS operational</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#1B3A5C;color:#fff">Phase 1-2</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td><strong>Protective order tier schema + PCES enforcement — 4a</strong></td><td>PCES/AEGIS operational · GC-4 role matrix finalized</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#1B3A5C;color:#fff">Phase 1-2</span></td></tr>
 <tr style="background:#E6F5F3"><td colspan="3" style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#0D6B5E;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;padding:5px 10px">Phase 3-4</td></tr>

 <tr><td>MOIRAI reverse DAG traversal — 1</td><td>MOIRAI provenance graph operational</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff">Phase 3-4</span></td></tr>

 <tr><td>Query-type authorization — 3c</td><td>PGS Phase 1-2 + classifier build · GC-3</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff">Phase 3-4</span></td></tr>

 <tr><td style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:600">⚠ Privileged MIMIR Channel security review</td><td>Architecture review + GC-2 resolved</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#8B2222;color:#fff">Gate</span></td></tr>

 <tr><td>Classification log generation — 4b</td><td>PCES tier model + evidence object model operational</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#9A6800;color:#fff">Phase 5-6</span></td></tr>

<!-- ── GC OPEN ITEMS ──────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec">Open Items Requiring General Counsel / Inspector General Input</h3>
<div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px">

 <div class="ac-notice" style="background:#FBF0F0;border:1px solid #E8B4B4">
  <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#8B2222;margin-bottom:6px">GC-1 — Derived Information Remediation Procedure · Needed before Phase 3-4</div>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:#6B1818;line-height:1.65;margin:0">THEMIS can enumerate the blast radius of a derived information event and block export until supervisor review. It cannot remediate the fact that synthesis was read prior to revocation. In IC oversight frameoversight framework Rule 5 inadvertent disclosure recovery scenarios, the agency's obligations — whether to notify the Inspector General, file corrections, or report to oversight — must be defined as a firm procedure before Phase 3-4 implementation. THEMIS implements whatever workflow General Counsel / Inspector General specifies; the workflow itself is a professional judgment the platform does not make.</p>
 </div>

 <div class="ac-notice" style="background:#FFF7E8;border:1px solid #E0C880">
  <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#9A6800;margin-bottom:6px">GC-2 — Gap Indicator Disclosure Acceptability · Needed before Phase 5-6</div>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:#7A5000;line-height:1.65;margin:0">The Gap Indicator communicates that relevant inaccessible evidence exists without revealing its content. In some matter contexts, even the existence of certain evidence may be sensitive. General Counsel / Inspector General must determine whether the gap indicator is appropriate in all requirement classes, or whether it should be configurable as disabled for specific high-sensitivity requirement types. This determination must occur before RQS gap coverage threshold configuration is finalized.</p>
 </div>

 <div class="ac-notice" style="background:#EEEBFF;border:1px solid #C4B5FD">
  <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#4A2FA8;margin-bottom:6px">GC-3 — Query-Type Authorization Taxonomy · Needed before Phase 3-4 classifier training</div>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:#3A2080;line-height:1.65;margin:0">The default query-type authorization table is a starting point based on general tradecraft compliance principles. The appropriate taxonomy for this firm's specific practice areas, risk profile, and supervision structure requires General Counsel / Inspector General determination in collaboration with lead analysts. The classifier cannot be trained until this taxonomy is finalized.</p>
 </div>

 <div class="ac-notice" style="background:#E6F5F3;border:1px solid #A0D0C4">
  <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#0D6B5E;margin-bottom:6px">GC-4 — Within-Matter Role-Tier Access Policy · Needed before Phase 1-2 tier deployment</div>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:#0A4A40;line-height:1.65;margin:0">The role-tier access matrix above shows default mappings. The agency's specific role taxonomy — how associates, intelligence analyst (junior)s, intelligence collection specialists, and other staff roles are defined, which roles can access which protective order tiers, and the process for granting TS_SCI_AEO and TS_SCI_SAP individual designation — are firm policy decisions requiring General Counsel / Inspector General input with HR and practice group leadership. These decisions must be finalized before the tier model deploys in Phase 1-2, because protective order tier is tagged at evidence ingestion. Retroactive re-classification is operationally expensive.</p>
 </div>

</div>
</div>

<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     SECTION 14 — MEMORY ARCHITECTURE (ADDENDUM C)
══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<div class="section" id="memory">
<div class="section-title">Memory Architecture</div>
<div class="section-sub">Addendum C — MOS/SAGA Memory Orchestration Service · MEMORY source type badge · Three operational concerns.</div>

<style>
.mem-badge{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:4px;border:1.5px solid;font-family:'SF Mono',Consolas,monospace;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em}
.mem-sub{font-size:9px;font-weight:700;background:rgba(0,0,0,.15);padding:1px 5px;border-radius:3px;letter-spacing:.06em}
.mem-schema{background:#F4F6FA;color:#1A2A3A;padding:14px 18px;border-radius:6px;border:1px solid var(--border);font-family:'SF Mono',Consolas,monospace;font-size:10.5px;line-height:1.65;overflow:auto;margin:10px 0 14px}
.mem-schema .kw{color:#1E40AF}.mem-schema .ty{color:#0D6B5E}.mem-schema .cm{color:#5A6480;font-style:italic}.mem-schema .st{color:#9A6800}
.mem-card{border-radius:8px;padding:14px 16px;margin-bottom:12px}
</style>

<!-- Governing principle -->
<div style="background:#F0EDFF;border-radius:8px;padding:16px 22px;border:1px solid #C4B5FD;margin-bottom:24px">
 <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#3B2A6B;margin-bottom:6px">The Governing Principle</div>
 <p style="font-size:12.5px;color:#2A1A5B;line-height:1.65;margin:0">Memory must be access-controlled at the same level as the evidence it was derived from. Memory derived from TS_SCI_AEO evidence is itself TS_SCI_AEO. Memory is not a bypass mechanism for the protective order tier model. Memory artifacts appear in the <code style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.15);padding:1px 5px;border-radius:3px">AccessRevocationEvent</code> blast radius when source evidence access is revoked. These constraints are architecturally enforced — not documented conventions.</p>
</div>

<!-- Memory type taxonomy -->
<h3 class="subsec">Memory Type Taxonomy</h3>
<table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:24px">
 <tr><th>Type</th><th>Description</th><th>Owner</th><th>Phase</th></tr>
 <tr><td><strong>Session working memory</strong></td><td>Active context window. Already handled by inference architecture.</td><td>Inference Gateway</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Live</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td><strong>Cross-session continuity</strong></td><td>Session summaries enabling analysts to resume without re-establishing context.</td><td>MOS/SAGA</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Phase 3-4</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td><strong>Matter knowledge layer</strong></td><td>Graduated confirmed claims forming a matter-specific corpus distinct from raw evidence.</td><td>MOS/SAGA</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#FFF7E8;color:#9A6800">Phase 5-6</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td><strong>Agent task memory</strong></td><td>Persistent state for multi-step STOA and PYTHIA tasks. Redis during execution, MOIRAI at milestones.</td><td>MOS/SAGA</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#EEEBFF;color:#4A2FA8">Year 2</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td><strong>Calibration memory</strong></td><td>TCS/MIMIR analyst accuracy patterns and FGTS correction corpus. Already operational.</td><td>TCS/MIMIR</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#E6F5F3;color:#0D6B5E">Live</span></td></tr>
</table>

<!-- MOS/SAGA service card -->
<h3 class="subsec">MOS/SAGA — Memory Orchestration Service</h3>
<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div>
  <p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65;margin-bottom:12px">SAGA in Norse mythology is the Ásynja associated with seeing, recounting, and preserving history — keeper of the accumulated record. MOS/SAGA owns the logic of what memory means. MOIRAI owns the record.</p>
  <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #3B2A6B;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px">
   <h4 style="color:#3B2A6B;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:6px">What MOS/SAGA owns</h4>
   <ul style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);padding-left:16px;line-height:1.65">
    <li>Session summary lifecycle — generation, versioning, MOIRAI storage, retrieval</li>
    <li>Matter knowledge layer — graduation logic, conflict resolution, MIMIR indexing</li>
    <li>Agent task memory — Redis task state, MOIRAI milestone promotion, handoff validation</li>
    <li>MEMORY source type metadata — provenance attribution for ATHENA badges</li>
   </ul>
  </div>
  <div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid var(--muted);padding:12px 14px">
   <h4 style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px;margin-bottom:6px">What MOS/SAGA does not own</h4>
   <ul style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);padding-left:16px;line-height:1.65">
    <li><strong>Storage</strong> — MOIRAI holds all memory artifacts as signed events</li>
    <li><strong>Access control</strong> — PCES/AEGIS enforces tier access on memory retrieval</li>
    <li><strong>Confidence scoring</strong> — TCS/MIMIR calibrates MEMORY-sourced claims</li>
    <li><strong>Blast radius enumeration</strong> — MOIRAI reverse DAG reaches MOS/SAGA artifacts through derivation edges MOS/SAGA writes at creation</li>
   </ul>
  </div>
 </div>
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:8px">Integration Map</h4>
  <table class="decision-table" style="margin:0">
   <tr><th>Service</th><th>Role</th></tr>
   <tr><td>MOIRAI</td><td>Receives all memory artifacts as signed events. Blast radius traversal via derivation edges.</td></tr>
   <tr><td>PCES/AEGIS</td><td>Enforces tier access on memory retrieval. Memory inherits most restrictive tier of contributing evidence.</td></tr>
   <tr><td>FGTS/ALETHEIA</td><td>Provides verified corrections that drive assessment knowledge graduation.</td></tr>
   <tr><td>ERAS/LOGOS</td><td>Provides session analytics that inform summary generation.</td></tr>
   <tr><td>TCS/MIMIR</td><td>Calibrates MEMORY-sourced claim confidence as a new source dimension.</td></tr>
   <tr><td>RQS/HERMES</td><td>Indexes assessment knowledge layer as a distinct retrieval source.</td></tr>
   <tr><td>STOA / PYTHIA</td><td>Consume MOS/SAGA for agent task memory during research orchestration.</td></tr>
   <tr><td>ATHENA</td><td>Surfaces MEMORY badge + session summary loading UI + task state viewer.</td></tr>
  </table>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- ── MEMORY SOURCE TYPE BADGE ──────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec">MEMORY Source Type Badge</h3>
<div class="card" style="border-left:4px solid #3B2A6B;padding:12px 16px;margin-bottom:16px">
 <p style="font-size:12.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.65;margin:0">A claim from a prior session summary is not GRND even if the original claim was GRND. It passed through a summarization step with its own error rate. The MEMORY badge makes this derivation visible — exactly as the TRANSCRIPT badge makes the ASR derivation step visible. The verification action is different, the confidence ceiling is different, and the FGTS routing is different.</p>
</div>

<!-- Badge display -->
<div style="display:flex;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:20px;align-items:flex-start">
 <div style="text-align:center">
  <div class="mem-badge" style="color:#3B2A6B;border-color:#3B2A6B;background:#EDE8FF">MEMORY <span class="mem-sub" style="background:#3B2A6B">SES</span></div>
  <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px">Session memory</div>
 </div>
 <div style="text-align:center">
  <div class="mem-badge" style="color:#0D5A3D;border-color:#0D5A3D;background:#DFFFEE">MEMORY <span class="mem-sub" style="background:#0D5A3D">MKL</span></div>
  <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px">Matter knowledge</div>
 </div>
 <div style="text-align:center">
  <div class="mem-badge" style="color:#6A3500;border-color:#6A3500;background:#FFF3E0">MEMORY <span class="mem-sub" style="background:#6A3500">TSK</span></div>
  <div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:4px">Task finding</div>
 </div>
 <div style="flex:1;min-width:200px;background:var(--bg);border-radius:6px;padding:10px 14px;font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6">
  Sub-type suffix is visible in the badge itself — analysts can distinguish memory sources at a glance. Expanding the badge shows: origin session date (SES), confirmation weight (MKL), or task objective and sub-task (TSK). All three link to the originating MOIRAI event.
 </div>
</div>

<!-- Badge detail table -->
<table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:20px">
 <tr><th>Badge</th><th>Source</th><th>Confidence ceiling</th><th>Primary error mode</th></tr>
 <tr>
  <td><span class="mem-badge" style="color:#3B2A6B;border-color:#3B2A6B;background:#EDE8FF;font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px">MEMORY <span class="mem-sub" style="background:#3B2A6B;font-size:8px">SES</span></span></td>
  <td>Prior session summary loaded by analyst</td>
  <td>Moderate reduction — summarization error rate</td>
  <td>Summarization compression, selective retention</td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <td><span class="mem-badge" style="color:#0D5A3D;border-color:#0D5A3D;background:#DFFFEE;font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px">MEMORY <span class="mem-sub" style="background:#0D5A3D;font-size:8px">MKL</span></span></td>
  <td>Graduated confirmed claim from assessment knowledge layer</td>
  <td>Small reduction — multiple confirmations required</td>
  <td>Graduation threshold errors, conflict artifacts</td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <td><span class="mem-badge" style="color:#6A3500;border-color:#6A3500;background:#FFF3E0;font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px">MEMORY <span class="mem-sub" style="background:#6A3500;font-size:8px">TSK</span></span></td>

  <td>Substantial reduction — agent intermediate findings</td>
  <td>Agent reasoning errors, incomplete sub-tasks</td>
 </tr>

<!-- Verification actions -->
<h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:10px">Verification Action Sets</h4>
<div class="grid grid-3" style="gap:10px;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div class="card" style="border-top:3px solid #3B2A6B;padding:10px 12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#3B2A6B;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">MEMORY·SES</div>
  <ul style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);padding-left:14px;line-height:1.65">
   <li><strong>Confirmed in original session</strong> — routes to FGTS as session memory confirmation</li>
   <li><strong>Not in original session</strong> — summary accuracy failure; routes to MOS/SAGA</li>
   <li><strong>Contradicts current evidence</strong> — high-value FGTS signal; prior confirmed claim invalidated</li>
   <li><strong>Dismiss</strong> — logged</li>
  </ul>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:3px solid #0D5A3D;padding:10px 12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#0D5A3D;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">MEMORY·MKL</div>
  <ul style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);padding-left:14px;line-height:1.65">
   <li><strong>Confirmed as established fact</strong> — increases confirmation weight</li>
   <li><strong>No longer accurate</strong> — routes to MOS/SAGA for invalidation + TVS/KAIROS</li>
   <li><strong>Disputed — supervisor review</strong> — both claims remain visible as disputed facts</li>
   <li><strong>Dismiss</strong> — logged</li>
  </ul>
 </div>
 <div class="card" style="border-top:3px solid #6A3500;padding:10px 12px">
  <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#6A3500;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-bottom:6px">MEMORY·TSK</div>
  <ul style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);padding-left:14px;line-height:1.65">
   <li><strong>Confirmed against current evidence</strong> — upgrades to FGTS-eligible correction</li>
   <li><strong>Task finding was incorrect</strong> — agent calibration signal + task state annotation</li>
   <li><strong>Dismiss</strong> — logged</li>
  </ul>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- ── THREE OPERATIONAL CONCERNS ────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec">Session Summary Lifecycle</h3>
<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:10px">Generated at session close or on analyst command. Structured — not narrative. Free-form summaries are easy to generate but impossible to retrieve precisely. Structured summaries with discrete fields are harder to generate but can be retrieved selectively.</p>
  <div class="mem-schema">
<span class="kw">interface</span> <span class="ty">SessionSummary</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;summary_id:     <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;session_id:     <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;matter_id:      <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;version:        <span class="ty">number</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;session_date:   <span class="ty">Timestamp</span>;<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="cm">// Structured fields — not narrative</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;verified_claims:    <span class="ty">VerifiedClaim</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;open_questions:     <span class="ty">string</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;evidence_reviewed:  <span class="ty">UUID</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;key_findings:       <span class="ty">Finding</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;next_steps:         <span class="ty">string</span>[];<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="cm">// Tier — most restrictive of session evidence</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;effective_tier:     <span class="ty">ProtectiveOrderTier</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;signed:             <span class="kw">true</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;prev_event_hash:    <span class="ty">SHA256</span>;<br>
}
  </div>
 </div>
 <div>
  <div style="background:#E6F5F3;border:1px solid #A0D0C4;border-radius:6px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px">
   <h4 style="color:#0D6B5E;font-size:11.5px;margin-bottom:5px">Explicit loading — not automatic injection</h4>
   <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:#0A4A40;line-height:1.6;margin:0">Session summary loading is analyst-controlled. The analyst sees the "Prior Sessions" panel, chooses what to load, and sees exactly which MEMORY·SES badges are active. Automatic context injection without analyst awareness conflicts with ATHENA's trust calibration philosophy.</p>
  </div>
  <div style="background:#EDE8FF;border:1px solid #C4B5FD;border-radius:6px;padding:12px 14px">
   <h4 style="color:#3B2A6B;font-size:11.5px;margin-bottom:5px">Versioning</h4>
   <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:#2A1A5B;line-height:1.6;margin:0">Summaries can be revised if new verification outcomes arrive after generation. MOS/SAGA maintains a version chain per session — linked MOIRAI events. ATHENA shows the most recent version with access to history.</p>
  </div>
 </div>
</div>

<h3 class="subsec">Matter Knowledge Layer</h3>
<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:10px">Matter-specific corpus of graduated confirmed claims — distinct from raw evidence, surfaced as a separate MIMIR retrieval source. Where raw evidence answers "what do the documents say", the assessment knowledge layer answers "what has this team established".</p>
  <h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">Graduation Threshold</h4>
  <table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:10px">
   <tr><th>Condition</th><th>Required?</th></tr>
   <tr><td>Supervisory acknowledgment</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Mandatory</span></td></tr>
   <tr><td>Domain RAI above threshold</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Mandatory</span></td></tr>
   <tr><td>High FGTS correction weight</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Mandatory</span></td></tr>
   <tr><td>No active dispute flag</td><td><span style="color:#8B2222;font-weight:700">Mandatory</span></td></tr>
   <tr><td>Peer agreement (second analyst)</td><td><span style="color:#9A6800;font-weight:600">Recommended</span></td></tr>
  </table>
  <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted)">Configurable by requirement class. Intelligence Oversight Board approval required for threshold changes.</p>
 </div>
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">Conflict Resolution</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:10px">When two analysts produce contradicting graduated claims, MOS/SAGA routes to the matter supervisor. Automatic resolution would silently lose information. Both claims remain visible as disputed facts during supervisor review.</p>
  <h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">MIMIR Indexing</h4>
  <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6">Graduated entries indexed in MIMIR as a named retrieval source distinct from raw evidence. Analysts can specify evidence-only, matter-knowledge-only, or both. RQS/HERMES tracks assessment knowledge retrieval quality separately.</p>
 </div>
</div>

<h3 class="subsec">Agent Task Memory</h3>
<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6;margin-bottom:10px">Redis during execution, MOIRAI at milestones. Active task state needs fast read/write. MOIRAI needs the audit record. The two operational modes are different substrates serving different purposes.</p>
  <div class="mem-schema">
<span class="kw">interface</span> <span class="ty">TaskState</span> {<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;task_id:      <span class="ty">UUID</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;task_type:    <span class="st">'stoa_research'</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;            | <span class="st">'pythia_analysis'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;status:       <span class="st">'active'</span> | <span class="st">'paused'</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;            | <span class="st">'completed'</span> | <span class="st">'handed_off'</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;sub_tasks:    <span class="ty">SubTask</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;open_questions: <span class="ty">string</span>[];<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;findings:     <span class="ty">Finding</span>[];<br>
<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;<span class="cm">// Access control</span><br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;credential_snapshot: <span class="ty">AccessScope</span>;<br>
&nbsp;&nbsp;credential_valid_until: <span class="ty">Timestamp</span>;<br>
}
  </div>
 </div>
 <div>
  <div style="background:#FBF0F0;border:1px solid #E8B4B4;border-radius:6px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px">
   <h4 style="color:#8B2222;font-size:11.5px;margin-bottom:5px">Credential re-validation on every resume</h4>
   <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:#6B1818;line-height:1.6;margin:0">Access credentials are validated on every task resume, not only at initiation. If analyst access changes while a task is running, the task is suspended immediately. It cannot resume until access is re-validated or scope is adjusted.</p>
  </div>
  <div style="background:#EDE8FF;border:1px solid #C4B5FD;border-radius:6px;padding:12px 14px">
   <h4 style="color:#3B2A6B;font-size:11.5px;margin-bottom:5px">Task handoff access validation</h4>
   <p style="font-size:11.5px;color:#2A1A5B;line-height:1.6;margin:0">On handoff to another analyst, MOS/SAGA validates the receiving analyst has access to all evidence referenced in task state — including evidence from completed sub-tasks. If any is out of scope, handoff is blocked until resolved.</p>
  </div>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- ── ACCESS CONTROL + BLAST RADIUS ─────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec">Access Control Integration</h3>
<div class="grid grid-2" style="gap:16px;margin-bottom:20px">
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">Tier Inheritance</h4>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.6">Every memory artifact inherits the most restrictive protective order tier of any evidence contributing to it. Computed by MOS/SAGA at creation time from PCES/AEGIS tier records. PCES enforces at retrieval. If contributing evidence tier changes, MOS/SAGA receives an <code>AccessRevocationEvent</code> and recomputes effective_tier for affected artifacts.</p>
 </div>
 <div>
  <h4 style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:var(--navy);margin-bottom:6px">Blast Radius — No New Logic Required</h4>
  <p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--sub);line-height:1.55;margin-bottom:8px">MOIRAI reverse DAG traversal reaches memory artifacts through derivation edges MOS/SAGA writes at creation:</p>
  <div style="font-size:11.5px;color:var(--sub);background:var(--bg);border-radius:5px;padding:8px 12px;font-family:'SF Mono',Consolas,monospace;line-height:1.7">
   session_summary <em>derived_from</em> session<br>
   session <em>retrieved</em> evidence_id<br>
   matter_knowledge_entry <em>derived_from</em> correction<br>
   correction <em>derived_from</em> session
  </div>
  <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--muted);margin-top:6px">Same blast radius marking and dissemination gate hardening applies to memory artifacts as to documents and reasoning records.</p>
 </div>
</div>

<!-- ── SEQUENCING ─────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<h3 class="subsec">Phase Sequencing</h3>
<table class="decision-table" style="margin-bottom:20px">
 <tr><th>Capability</th><th>Depends on</th><th>Phase</th></tr>
 <tr style="background:#EEF2F8"><td colspan="3" style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#1B3A5C;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;padding:5px 10px">Phase 3-4</td></tr>
 <tr><td>Session summary schema (co-design with MOIRAI)</td><td>MOIRAI Phase 3-4 concurrent build</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff">Phase 3-4</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Session summary generation + MOIRAI storage</td><td>MOIRAI provenance graph operational</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff">Phase 3-4</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>Session summary retrieval + ATHENA UI</td><td>Summaries in MOIRAI</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff">Phase 3-4</span></td></tr>
 <tr><td>MEMORY·SES badge + TCS calibration stream</td><td>Session summary retrieval live</td><td><span class="badge" style="background:#0D6B5E;color:#fff">Phase 3-4</span></td></tr>
 <tr style="background:#FFF7E8"><td colspan="3" style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#9A6800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;padding:5px 10px">Phase 5-6</td></tr>
